# W 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 2,248 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-w-000001"></a>
## w

*n.* 1. **鎢, 鎢錳鐵礦** — a heavy grey-white metallic element; the pure form is used mainly in electrical applications; it is found in several ores including wolframite and scheelite
2. **西, 西方** — the cardinal compass point that is a 270 degrees
3. **瓦特** — a unit of power equal to 1 joule per second; the power dissipated by a current of 1 ampere flowing across a resistance of 1 ohm
4. the 23rd letter of the Roman alphabet

<a id="entry-w-000002"></a>
## w-shaped

*adj.* 1. **W形狀+的** — shaped in the form of the letter W

<a id="entry-w-000003"></a>
## w. h. auden

*n.* 1. **奧登，威·於** — United States poet (born in England) (1907-1973)

<a id="entry-w-000004"></a>
## Wa

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **佤族, 佤** — An ethnic group in Myanmar and China.

<a id="entry-w-000005"></a>
## Wa State

*prop. n.* 1. **佤邦** — An officially unrecognised state in Myanmar, part of northern Shan State.

<a id="entry-w-000006"></a>
## Waalwijk

*prop. n.* 1. **瓦爾韋克 /瓦爾韋克** — A city and municipality of North Brabant, Netherlands.

<a id="entry-w-000007"></a>
## wacky

*adj.* 1. **可笑+的, 莫名其妙, 古怪+的, 滑稽+的, 荒謬+的** — ludicrous, foolish
*“gave me a cockamamie reason for not going”*
*“wore a goofy hat”*
2. **乖張反常+的, 癲狂+的, 發狂+的, 發瘋+的, 瘋狂+的** — informal or slang terms for mentally irregular
*“it used to drive my husband balmy”*

<a id="entry-w-000008"></a>
## wad

*v.* 1. compress into a wad
*“wad paper into the box”*
2. crowd or pack to capacity
*“the theater was jampacked”*
*n.* 1. **書庫, 批, 堆** — (often followed by `of') a large number or amount or extent
*“a batch of letters”*
*“a deal of trouble”*
2. a small mass of soft material
*“he used a wad of cotton to wipe the counter”*
3. a wad of something chewable as tobacco

<a id="entry-w-000009"></a>
## Wadden Sea

*prop. n.* 1. **瓦登海** — A shallow and narrow sea peripheral to the North Sea, situated between the Frisian Islands and the mainland coasts of Denmark, Germany and the Netherlands.

<a id="entry-w-000010"></a>
## wade

**發音：** /weɪd/
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **涉水** — To walk through water or something that impedes progress.
2. walk (through relatively shallow water)
*“Can we wade across the river to the other side?”*
*“Wade the pond”*
*n.* 1. English tennis player who won many women's singles titles (born in 1945)

<a id="entry-w-000011"></a>

*prop. n.* 1. **威妥瑪拼音 /威妥瑪拼音, 威翟式拼音, 韋氏拼音 /韋氏拼音** — A system for transcribing the Beijing dialect of Mandarin Chinese into the Latin alphabet; formally uses hyphens and the spiritus asper apostrophe.

## wader

*n.* 1. **涉水鳥, 涉禽, 涉禽類** — any of many long-legged birds that wade in water in search of food

## wading bird

*n.* 1. **涉禽** — Any of various long-legged birds, such as cranes (Gruidae), herons (Ardeidae), storks (Ciconiidae), or flamingos (Phoenicopteridae), that wade in shallow water in search of food.
2. **涉水鳥, 涉禽, 涉禽類** — any of many long-legged birds that wade in water in search of food

## wads

*“she amassed stacks of newspapers”*

<a id="entry-w-000016"></a>
## wafer

**發音：** /ˈweɪ̯fəː/
*n.* 1. **封緘紙** — a small adhesive disk of paste; used to seal letters
2. **威化餅 /威化餅** — A light, thin, flat biscuit/cookie.
3. **晶圓 /晶圓** — A thin disk of silicon or other semiconductor on which an electronic circuit is produced.
4. a small thin crisp cake or cookie
5. thin disk of unleavened bread used in a religious service (especially in the celebration of the Eucharist)

*adj.* 1. **極薄+的** — very thin
*“wafer-thin sheets of metal”*
*adv.* 1. very thin
*“it was cut wafer-thin”*

2. pancake batter baked in a waffle iron
*v.* 1. **不願, 猶豫, 動搖, 猶豫不決, 躊躇** — pause or hold back in uncertainty or unwillingness

## waft

*v.* 1. **使飄蕩, 吹送, 漂送** — blow gently
*“A breeze wafted through the door”*
2. be driven or carried along, as by the air
*“Sounds wafted into the room”*
*n.* 1. a long flag; often tapering

<a id="entry-w-000021"></a>
## wag

**發音：** /wæɡ/
*v.* 1. *(Australia, New-Zealand, UK)* **逃學 /逃學, 曠課 /曠課, 逃課 /逃課, 翹課 /翹課** — To play truant from school.
2. move from side to side
*“The happy dog wagged his tail”*
*n.* 1. **搖動, 搖擺** — causing to move repeatedly from side to side
2. a witty amusing person who makes jokes

<a id="entry-w-000022"></a>
## wage

**發音：** /weɪd͡ʒ/
*n.* 1. **作報償的東西, 工資, 報酬, 薪水, 薪金** — something that remunerates
*“wages were paid by check”*
*“he wasted his pay on drink”*
2. *(often, plural)* **工資 /工資, 薪金, 薪水, 工錢 /工錢** — An amount of money paid to a worker for a specified quantity of work, usually calculated on an hourly basis and expressed in an amount of money per hour.
*v.* 1. **使從事於, 開展, 發動** — carry on (wars, battles, or campaigns)
*“Napoleon and Hitler waged war against all of Europe”*
2. *(transitive)* **發動 /發動, 發起 /發起** — To conduct or carry out (a war or other contest).

<a id="entry-w-000030"></a>
## wage-earning

*adj.* 1. **工人階級+的, 工薪階層** — working for hourly wages rather than fixed (e.g. annual) salaries
*“working-class occupations include manual as well as industrial labor”*
2. of those who work for wages especially manual or industrial laborers

## wager

**發音：** /ˈweɪ.d͡ʒə(ɹ)/

*“he did it on a bet”*
2. **賭 /賭** — A bet; a stake; a pledge.
3. **賭注 /賭注** — The subject of a bet.
4. **賭物, 賭金, 賭注** — the money risked on a gamble

*“I bet $100 on that new horse”*
*“She played all her money on the dark horse”*
2. **打賭, 確信** — maintain with or as if with a bet
*“I bet she will be there!”*

<a id="entry-w-000052"></a>
## wait

**發音：** /weɪt/
*v.* 1. **等, 等候** — stay in one place and anticipate or expect something
*“I had to wait on line for an hour to get the tickets”*
2. **候, 待, 等待, 等** — wait before acting
*“the scientists held off announcing their results until they repeated the experiment”*
3. **伺候用餐, 當服務員** — serve as a waiter or waitress in a restaurant
*“I'm waiting on tables at Maxim's”*
4. *(intransitive)* **等, 等待, 待, 候** — To delay movement or action until some event or time; to remain neglected or in readiness.
5. look forward to the probable occurrence of
*“We were expecting a visit from our relatives”*
*“She is looking to a promotion”*
*n.* 1. **延遲, 耽擱, 耽擱的時間** — time during which some action is awaited
*“instant replay caused too long a delay”*
*“he ordered a hold in the action”*
2. the act of waiting (remaining inactive in one place while expecting something)
*“the wait was an ordeal for him”*

<a id="entry-w-000053"></a>
## wait for the other shoe to drop

*v.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **提心吊膽 /提心吊膽** — To await a seemingly inevitable event, especially one that is not desirable.

*“May I serve you?”*
*“She attends the old lady in the wheelchair”*
2. **侍候, 服侍, 侍奉, 伺候** — To provide a service to (someone); to act as a servant to (someone); to serve (someone) as a waiter or waitress in a restaurant.

<a id="entry-w-000055"></a>

<a id="entry-w-000063"></a>
## waive

*v.* 1. **不堅持, 棄絕, 擯絕, 拋棄, 放棄** — do without or cease to hold or adhere to
*“We are dispensing with formalities”*
*“relinquish the old ideas”*
2. **拋棄, 放棄** — lose (s.th.) or lose the right to (s.th.) by some error, offense, or crime
*“you've forfeited your right to name your successor”*
*“forfeited property”*

<a id="entry-w-000064"></a>
## waiver

**發音：** /ˈweɪ.və(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. **棄權 /棄權** — The act of waiving, or not insisting on, some right, claim, or privilege.
2. **棄權書, 棄權證書, 解僱, 豁免, 免除** — a formal written statement of relinquishment
3. **棄權證書 /棄權證書** — A legal document removing some requirement, such as waiving a right (giving it up) or a waiver of liability (agreeing to hold someone blameless).

<a id="entry-w-000065"></a>
## waka

*n.* 1. **和歌** — A kind of classical Japanese poem.

<a id="entry-w-000066"></a>
## Wakayama

*prop. n.* 1. **和歌山** — A prefecture of Japan.

<a id="entry-w-000067"></a>
## wake

**發音：** /ˈweɪ̯k/
*n.* 1. **後果** — the consequences of an event (especially a catastrophic event)
*“the aftermath of war”*
*“in the wake of the accident no one knew how many had been injured”*
2. **守夜** — A period after a person's death before or after the body is buried, cremated, etc.; in some cultures accompanied by a party or collectively sorting through the deceased's personal effects.
3. **尾流** — The turbulent air left behind a flying aircraft.
4. **守靈** — a vigil held over a corpse the night before burial
*“there's no weeping at an Irish wake”*
5. an island in the western Pacific between Guam and Hawaii
6. the wave that spreads behind a boat as it moves forward
*“the motorboat's wake capsized the canoe”*
*v.* 1. **喚醒, 醒, 醒來** — stop sleeping
*“She woke up to the sound of the alarm clock”*
2. be awake, be alert, be there
3. arouse or excite feelings and passions
*“The ostentatious way of living of the rich ignites the hatred of the poor”*
*“The refugees' fate stirred up compassion around the world”*
4. make aware of
*“His words woke us to terrible facts of the situation”*
5. cause to become awake or conscious
*“He was roused by the drunken men in the street”*
*“Please wake me at 6 AM.”*

<a id="entry-w-000068"></a>

## Wake Island

*prop. n.* 1. **威克島 /威克島** — An island of the United States, among the islands of Micronesia in the Pacific Ocean, administered by the Office of Insular Affairs of the United States Department of the Interior and used solely by the United States Air Force.

<a id="entry-w-000070"></a>
## wake up

**發音：** /ˌweɪk ˈʌp/
*v.* 1. **喚醒, 醒, 醒來** — stop sleeping
*“She woke up to the sound of the alarm clock”*
2. *(intransitive)* **醒, 醒來 /醒來** — To stop sleeping; to awake.
3. *(transitive)* **喚醒 /喚醒, 叫醒** — To awaken somebody.
4. cause to become awake or conscious
*“He was roused by the drunken men in the street”*
*“Please wake me at 6 AM.”*

<a id="entry-w-000072"></a>
## wake-up call

*n.* 1. *(US, literally)* **叫醒電話 /叫醒電話, 叫醒服務 /叫醒服務** — A telephone call to awaken someone at a certain time, especially one requested by the person while staying at a hotel.
2. *(figuratively)* **當頭棒喝 /當頭棒喝** — An alert, reminder, or call to action caused by a dramatic event.
3. a warning to take action concerning something that was overlooked or neglected
*“the bombing was a wake-up call to strengthen domestic security”*
4. a telephone call that you request be made a specific time in order to wake you up at that time (especially in hotels)
*“she left a wake-up call for 7 a.m.”*

<a id="entry-w-000073"></a>
## wakefulness

*n.* 1. **失眠** — a temporary state in which you are unable (or unwilling) to sleep
*“accept your wakefulness and sleep in its own contrary way is more likely to come”*
2. a periodic state during which you are conscious and aware of the world
*“consciousness during wakefulness in a sane person is pretty well ordered and familiar”*
3. the process of paying close and continuous attention
*“wakefulness, watchfulness, and bellicosity make a good hunter”*
*“vigilance is especially susceptible to fatigue”*

<a id="entry-w-000074"></a>
## waken

*v.* 1. **喚醒, 醒, 醒來** — stop sleeping
*“She woke up to the sound of the alarm clock”*
2. cause to become awake or conscious
*“He was roused by the drunken men in the street”*
*“Please wake me at 6 AM.”*

## walk

**發音：** /wɔːk/
*v.* 1. **步行, 行走, 走, 走路** — use one's feet to advance; advance by steps
*“Walk, don't run!”*
*“We walked instead of driving”*
2. **慢步走, 散步** — walk at a pace
*“The horses walked across the meadow”*
3. **以一種特殊的方式指導或表現自, 處事** — live or behave in a specified manner
*“walk in sadness”*
4. accompany or escort
*“I'll walk you to your car”*
5. obtain a base on balls
6. traverse or cover by walking
*“Walk the tightrope”*
*“Paul walked the streets of Damascus”*
*n.* 1. **散步, 漫步** — A trip made by walking.
2. the act of traveling by foot
*“walking is a healthy form of exercise”*
3. (baseball) an advance to first base by a batter who receives four balls
*“he worked the pitcher for a base on balls”*
4. manner of walking
*“he had a funny walk”*
5. the act of walking somewhere
*“he took a walk after lunch”*
6. a path set aside for walking
*“after the blizzard he shoveled the front walk”*

<a id="entry-w-000081"></a>
## walk a tightrope

*v.* 1. *(figuratively)* **走鋼絲 /走鋼絲** — To undertake a precarious course of action.

*v.* 1. **遊蕩** — behave in a certain manner or have certain properties
*“He walks around with his nose in the air”*
*“She walks around with this strange boyfriend”*
2. walk with no particular goal
*“we were walking around in the garden”*
*“after breakfast, she walked about in the park”*
3. walk around something
4. walk randomly
*“We were walking around in the neighborhood to see whether we could find an open drugstore”*

<a id="entry-w-000083"></a>

## wander

**發音：** /ˈwɒndə/
*v.* 1. **遊蕩, 漫遊, 流浪, 徘徊, 漫步** — move about aimlessly or without any destination, often in search of food or employment
*“The gypsies roamed the woods”*
*“roving vagabonds”*
2. **不忠, 使戴綠帽子, 背叛** — be sexually unfaithful to one's partner in marriage
*“She cheats on her husband”*
*“Might her husband be wandering?”*
3. *(intransitive)* **漫步, 徘徊** — To move without purpose or specified destination; often in search of livelihood.
4. **漫步, 徘徊** — go via an indirect route or at no set pace
*“After dinner, we wandered into town”*
5. **流浪, 離題, 迷失方向, 迷路** — lose clarity or turn aside especially from the main subject of attention or course of argument in writing, thinking, or speaking
*“She always digresses when telling a story”*
*“her mind wanders”*
6. to move or cause to move in a sinuous, spiral, or circular course
*“the river winds through the hills”*
*“the path meanders through the vineyards”*

<a id="entry-w-000149"></a>
## wandering

*adj.* 1. **徘徊+的, 漫遊+的, 流浪+的** — migratory
*“a restless mobile society”*
*“the nomadic habits of the Bedouins”*
2. **遊移+的, 不穩定+的, 漂泊+的** — having no fixed course
*“an erratic comet”*
*“his life followed a wandering course”*
3. of a path e.g.
*“meandering streams”*
*“rambling forest paths”*
*n.* 1. travelling about without any clear destination
*“she followed him in his wanderings and looked after him”*

<a id="entry-w-000150"></a>
## wandering albatross

*n.* 1. **漂泊信天翁** — very large albatross; white with wide black wings

2. very strong or irresistible impulse to travel

<a id="entry-w-000152"></a>
## wane

*v.* 1. **衰微, 衰落, 衰退** — grow smaller
*“Interest in the project waned”*
2. **變小, 衰減, 衰落, 虧缺, 消逝** — become smaller
*“Interest in his novels waned”*
3. **虧缺, 缺損, 變小** — decrease in phase
*“the moon is waning”*
*n.* 1. **衰落, 衰退** — a gradual decline (in size or strength or power or number)

<a id="entry-w-000175"></a>
## want

*v.* 1. **希望, 想要, 願意, 願望, 要** — feel or have a desire for; want strongly
*“I want to go home now”*
*“I want my own room”*
2. **必要, 必須, 需, 要, 需要** — have need of
*“This piano wants the attention of a competent tuner”*
3. **缺乏, 缺少** — be without, lack; be deficient in
*“want courtesy”*
*“want the strength to go on living”*
4. hunt or look for; want for a particular reason
*“Your former neighbor is wanted by the FBI”*
*“Uncle Sam wants you”*
5. wish or demand the presence of
*“I want you here at noon!”*
*n.* 1. **窮困, 缺乏** — a state of extreme poverty
2. **不足, 欠缺的事物, 沒有, 短少, 需要** — the state of needing something that is absent or unavailable
*“there is a serious lack of insight into the problem”*
*“water is the critical deficiency in desert regions”*
3. anything that is necessary but lacking
*“he had sufficient means to meet his simple needs”*
*“I tried to supply his wants”*
4. a specific feeling of desire
*“he got his wish”*
*“he was above all wishing and desire”*

<a id="entry-w-000176"></a>
## wanted

**發音：** /ˈwɒn.tɪd/
*adj.* 1. **受通緝+的, 想要+的, 被通緝+的** — desired or wished for or sought
*“couldn't keep her eyes off the wanted toy”*
*“a wanted criminal”*
2. **通緝 /通緝** — Subject to immediate detainment by law enforcement authorities on sight.
3. characterized by feeling or showing fond affection for
*“a cherished friend”*
*“children are precious”*

<a id="entry-w-000177"></a>

2. a public announcement by a law enforcement agency that they desire to question or arrest some person

<a id="entry-w-000178"></a>
## wanton

**發音：** /ˈwɒntən/
*n.* 1. **淘氣鬼 /淘氣鬼, 淘氣包 /淘氣包** — An overly playful person; a trifler.
2. lewd or lascivious woman
*v.* 1. **虛度, 閒蕩, 鬼混, 浪費** — waste time; spend one's time idly or inefficiently
2. **嬉戲, 放肆** — indulge in a carefree or voluptuous way of life
3. **揮霍, 浪費** — spend wastefully
*“wanton one's money away”*
4. become extravagant; indulge (oneself) luxuriously
5. engage in amorous play
6. behave extremely cruelly and brutally
*adj.* 1. *(archaic)* **淘氣 /淘氣** — Undisciplined, unruly; not able to be controlled.
2. *(obsolete)* **淘氣 /淘氣** — Playful, sportive; merry or carefree.
3. **毫無約束+的, 放蕩+的, 淫蕩+的** — casual and unrestrained in sexual behavior
*“her easy virtue”*
*“he was told to avoid loose (or light) women”*
4. occurring without motivation or provocation
*“motiveless malignity”*
*“unprovoked and dastardly attack"- F.D.Roosevelt”*

<a id="entry-w-000179"></a>

2. **揮霍, 浪費** — spend wastefully
*“wanton one's money away”*

*“this young girl has to share a room with her mother who lives promiscuously”*

*“the animals were killed wantonly for sport”*

## war

*n.* 1. **戰爭, 衝突** — a legal state created by a declaration of war and ended by official declaration during which the international rules of war apply
*“war was declared in November but actual fighting did not begin until the following spring”*
2. **處於交戰狀態, 戰, 戰爭, 戰鬥, 鬥爭** — an active struggle between competing entities
*“a price war”*
*“a war of wits”*
3. **鬥爭** — a concerted campaign to end something that is injurious
*“the war on poverty”*
*“the war against crime”*
4. the waging of armed conflict against an enemy
*“thousands of people were killed in the war”*
*v.* 1. make or wage war

<a id="entry-w-000187"></a>

*n.* 1. **東部無敵駿馬** — thoroughbred that won the triple crown in 1937

*n.* 1. **美國內戰, 美國南北戰爭** — civil war in the United States between the North and the South; 1861-1865

<a id="entry-w-000189"></a>
## war bond

<a id="entry-w-000190"></a>
## war chest

*n.* 1. **戰爭基金** — a fund accumulated to finance a war (or a political campaign)

<a id="entry-w-000191"></a>

**發音：** /ˈwɔɹ ˈkɒm.jʊˌnɪzm̩/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **戰時共產主義 /戰時共產主義** — The economic policy of the Soviet Russia from 1918 to 1921 during the Russian Civil War (1917‒23) and the Allied intervention (1918‒25).

<a id="entry-w-000192"></a>
## war crime

**發音：** /ˈwɔː kɹaɪm/
*n.* 1. **戰爭犯罪, 戰爭罪, 戰爭罪行** — a crime committed in wartime; violation of rules of war
2. **戰爭罪 /戰爭罪** — A punishable offence under international law for violations of the laws of war by any person or persons, military or civilian.

<a id="entry-w-000193"></a>
## war criminal

*n.* 1. **戰犯 /戰犯** — A person guilty of one or more war crimes.
2. an offender who violates international law during times of war

<a id="entry-w-000194"></a>
## war dance

*n.* 1. **戰爭舞 /戰爭舞, 戰陣舞 /戰陣舞, 戰舞 /戰舞** — A ceremonial dance performed before a major battle or after a victory, usually by a tribe.
2. a ceremonial dance performed before a battle or after a victory

<a id="entry-w-000195"></a>
## war drum

*n.* 1. **戰鼓 /戰鼓** — A drum that is beaten to rally and encourage troops or to signify that war is approaching.

<a id="entry-w-000196"></a>
## war economy

*n.* 1. **戰爭經濟 /戰爭經濟** — A country's economy that has been reorganized to optimally support the war effort.

<a id="entry-w-000197"></a>
## war effort

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **抗戰 /抗戰** — A coordinated mobilisation of a country's resources, both industrial and human, in support of its military forces during a war.

<a id="entry-w-000198"></a>
## war game

*n.* 1. **軍事演習 /軍事演習** — A simulation (by whatever means, physical or digital), for training and strategy purposes, of a military operation involving two or more opposing forces using rules, data, and procedures designed to depict an actual or assumed real-life situation.
2. **戰爭遊戲 /戰爭遊戲** — A game, for entertainment or edutainment purposes, that simulates or represents a military operation.
3. a simulation of a military operation intended to train military commanders or to demonstrate a situation or to test a proposed strategy

<a id="entry-w-000199"></a>
## war hammer

*n.* 1. **戰錘 /戰錘** — A medieval hammerlike weapon used in close combat.

<a id="entry-w-000200"></a>
## war hero

*n.* 1. **戰鬥英雄 /戰鬥英雄** — Someone who commits an act of heroism in a combat setting.

<a id="entry-w-000201"></a>
## War of 1812

*prop. n.* 1. *(historical)* **1812年戰爭 /1812年戰爭** — A military conflict that lasted from 1812 to 1815, fought between the United States of America and the United Kingdom, its North American colonies, and its North American Indian allies.

<a id="entry-w-000202"></a>
## war of aggression

*n.* 1. **侵略戰爭 /侵略戰爭** — A military conflict waged without the justification of self-defense, usually for territorial gain and subjugation.

<a id="entry-w-000203"></a>
## war of american independence

*n.* 1. **美國獨立戰爭** — the revolution of the American Colonies against Great Britain; 1775-1783

<a id="entry-w-000204"></a>
## war of attrition

*n.* 1. **消耗戰 /消耗戰, 摩擦戰 /摩擦戰** — A prolonged war in which the warring sides try to defeat their opponents by wearing them out.

<a id="entry-w-000205"></a>
## war of nerves

*n.* 1. *(broadly, idiomatic)* **神經戰 /神經戰, 心理戰 /心理戰** — A situation in which opposed parties refrain from direct conflict but maintain a tense, contentious relationship in which each uses annoyances or intimidating psychological tactics to attempt to dishearten and unnerve the other.
2. the use of psychological tactics to destroy the opponents' morale

<a id="entry-w-000206"></a>
## war of words

*n.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **舌戰 /舌戰, 口水戰 /口水戰, 口水仗** — A heated exchange of threatening or inflammatory statements.

<a id="entry-w-000207"></a>
## War on Terrorism

*prop. n.* 1. *(US)* **反恐戰爭 /反恐戰爭** — An ongoing campaign by the United States (and some of its allies) the stated aim of which is to counter international terrorism, but especially to counter al-Qaeda and other militant Muslim groups.
2. **反恐戰爭 /反恐戰爭** — The common term for the military, political, legal and ideological conflict against Islamic terrorism, and specifically used in reference to operations by the United States, in response to the September 11, 2001 attacks.

<a id="entry-w-000208"></a>

*n.* 1. **化妝品** — cosmetics applied to the face to improve or change your appearance
2. adornment consisting of paint applied to the face and body of certain Amerindians before a battle
3. full ceremonial regalia

<a id="entry-w-000209"></a>

<a id="entry-w-000210"></a>

*n.* 1. **第一次世界大戰** — a war between the allies (Russia, France, British Empire, Italy, United States, Japan, Rumania, Serbia, Belgium, Greece, Portugal, Montenegro) and the Central Powers (Germany, Austria-Hungary, Turkey, Bulgaria) from 1914 to 1918

<a id="entry-w-000211"></a>

## ward

4. avert, turn away, or repel

## warm

*adj.* 1. **暖和, 溫暖+的** — having or producing a comfortable and agreeable degree of heat or imparting or maintaining heat
*“a warm body”*
*“a warm room”*
2. **溫暖+的, 熱心+的, 熱情+的** — psychologically warm; friendly and responsive
*“a warm greeting”*
*“a warm personality”*
3. **暖色+的** — (color) inducing the impression of warmth; used especially of reds and oranges and yellows
*“warm reds and yellows and orange”*
4. **充滿深情的, 摯愛的, 有深情的, 嫩+的, 不成熟+的** — having or displaying warmth or affection
*“affectionate children”*
*“a fond embrace”*
5. **熱情洋溢+的, 激烈+的, 熱烈+的, 熱心+的** — characterized by strong enthusiasm
*“ardent revolutionaries”*
*“warm support”*
6. **激烈+的** — characterized by liveliness or excitement or disagreement
*“a warm debate”*
*v.* 1. **變暖和, 使變暖** — get warm or warmer
*“The soup warmed slowly on the stove”*
2. **使變暖, 使溫暖, 加溫** — make warm or warmer
*“The blanket will warm you”*
*adv.* 1. **親切+地, 暖+地, 暖喝+地, 溫暖+地, 熱烈+地** — in a warm manner
*“warmly dressed”*
*“warm-clad skiers”*

<a id="entry-w-000249"></a>
## warn

**發音：** /wɔːn/
*v.* 1. **警告, 警戒** — notify of danger, potential harm, or risk
*“The director warned him that he might be fired”*
*“The doctor warned me about the dangers of smoking”*
2. *(transitive)* **警告, 提醒, 警戒** — To make (someone) aware of impending danger, evil, etc.
3. admonish or counsel in terms of someone's behavior
*“I warned him not to go too far”*
*“I warn you against false assumptions”*
4. ask to go away
*“The old man warned the children off his property”*
5. notify, usually in advance
*“I warned you that I would ask some difficult questions”*

<a id="entry-w-000250"></a>
## warning

*“a letter of admonition about the dangers of immorality”*
*“the warning was to beware of surprises”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **警告** — An instance of warning someone.
3. a message informing of danger
*“a warning that still more bombs could explode”*
4. notification of something, usually in advance
*“they gave little warning of their arrival”*
*“she had only had four days' warning before leaving Berlin”*
*adj.* 1. **警戒+的** — serving to warn

*“an exemplary jail sentence”*
*interj.* 1. **小心** — Used to warn of danger in signs and notices.

*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **保證 /保證** — To guarantee (something) to be (of a specified quality, value, etc.).
2. show to be reasonable or provide adequate ground for
*“The emergency does not warrant all of us buying guns”*
*“The end justifies the means”*
3. stand behind and guarantee the quality, accuracy, or condition of
*“The dealer warrants all the cars he sells”*
*“I warrant this information”*
*n.* 1. **搜查證, 逮捕證, 許可證** — a writ from a court commanding police to perform specified acts
2. **認股權證** — a type of security issued by a corporation (usually together with a bond or preferred stock) that gives the holder the right to purchase a certain amount of common stock at a stated price
*“as a sweetener they offered warrants along with the fixed-income securities”*
3. **保用, 保證, 保證書, 擔保** — a written assurance that some product or service will be provided or will meet certain specifications

5. formal and explicit approval
*“a Democrat usually gets the union's endorsement”*

## wash out

*v.* 1. **因下雨阻礙** — prevent or interrupt due to rain
*“The storm had washed out the game”*
2. wash free from unwanted substances, such as dirt
*“Wash out your dirty shirt in the sink”*
3. wear or destroy by the force of water
*“The hail storms had washed out the bridges”*
4. remove by the application of water or other liquid and soap or some other cleaning agent
*“he washed the dirt from his coat”*
*“The nurse washed away the blood”*
5. deplete of strength or vitality
*“The illness washed her out”*
6. drain off the color in the course of laundering
*“The harsh soap washed out the delicate blouse”*

<a id="entry-w-000288"></a>
## wash up

*v.* 1. *(US, intransitive)* **洗臉 /洗臉, 洗手** — To wash one's hands or face, often around mealtimes.
2. *(intransitive)* **沖刷** — To be carried by water to land.
3. wash one's face and hands
*“She freshened up in the bathroom”*
4. carry somewhere (of water or current or waves)
*“The tide washed up the corpse”*
5. wash dishes
*“I cook and my husband washes up after dinner”*
6. be carried somewhere by water or as if by water
*“The body washed up on the beach”*

<a id="entry-w-000319"></a>
## waste

**發音：** /ˈweɪ̯st/
*v.* 1. **損耗, 浪費, 虛耗, 消耗, 濫用** — spend thoughtlessly; throw away
*“He wasted his inheritance on his insincere friends”*
*“You squandered the opportunity to get and advanced degree”*
2. *(transitive)* **浪費 /浪費, 白費 /白費** — To squander (money or resources) uselessly; to spend (time) idly; to dissipate.
3. **損耗, 浪費** — get rid of
*“We waste the dirty water by channeling it into the sewer”*
4. **徑流, 流掉** — run off as waste
*“The water wastes back into the ocean”*
5. **揮霍無度, 揮霍, 浪費** — spend extravagantly
*“waste not, want not”*
6. **失去活力, 憔悴, 衰弱** — lose vigor, health, or flesh, as through grief
*“After her husband died, she just pined away”*
*n.* 1. **廢物, 垃圾** — any materials unused and rejected as worthless or unwanted
*“they collect the waste once a week”*
*“much of the waste material is carried off in the sewers”*
2. **浪費** — the trait of wasting resources
*“a life characterized by thriftlessness and waste”*
*“the wastefulness of missed opportunities”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **荒地** — A wasteland; an uninhabited desolate region; a wilderness or desert.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **浪費 /浪費** — The action or progress of wasting; extravagant consumption or ineffectual use.
5. useless or profitless activity; using or expending or consuming thoughtlessly or carelessly
*“if the effort brings no compensating gain it is a waste”*
*“mindless dissipation of natural resources”*
6. an uninhabited wilderness that is worthless for cultivation
*“the barrens of central Africa”*
*“the trackless wastes of the desert”*
*adj.* 1. located in a dismal or remote area; desolate
*“a desert island”*
*“a godforsaken wilderness crossroads”*

## watchman

**發音：** /ˈwɒtʃmən/
*n.* 1. **看守者** — A man set to watch: a man who keeps guard, especially one who guards a building, or the streets of a city, by night; (loosely) any such person of any sex or gender.
2. a guard who keeps watch

<a id="entry-w-000360"></a>
## watchtower

*n.* 1. **崗樓 /崗樓, 望樓 /望樓** — An observation tower in which a lookout keeps watch over prisoners, or looks out for fires, etc.
2. an observation tower for a lookout to watch over prisoners or watch for fires or enemies

<a id="entry-w-000361"></a>
## watchword

*n.* 1. **口令, 密碼, 暗號** — a secret word or phrase known only to a restricted group
*“he forgot the password”*
2. a slogan used to rally support for a cause
*“a cry to arms”*
*“our watchword will be `democracy'”*

<a id="entry-w-000362"></a>
## water

*n.* 1. **水** — binary compound that occurs at room temperature as a clear colorless odorless tasteless liquid; freezes into ice below 0 degrees centigrade and boils above 100 degrees centigrade; widely used as a solvent
2. **尿, 小便** — liquid excretory product
*“there was blood in his urine”*
*“the child had to make water”*
3. the part of the earth's surface covered with water (such as a river or lake or ocean)
*“they invaded our territorial waters”*
*“they were sitting by the water's edge”*
4. once thought to be one of four elements composing the universe (Empedocles)
5. a facility that provides a source of water
*“the town debated the purification of the water supply”*
*“first you have to cut off the water”*
6. a liquid necessary for the life of most animals and plants
*“he asked for a drink of water”*
*v.* 1. **注入水, 澆水於** — provide with water
*“We watered the buffalo”*
2. supply with water, as with channels or ditches or streams
*“Water the fields”*
3. secrete or form water, as tears or saliva
*“My mouth watered at the prospect of a good dinner”*
*“His eyes watered”*
4. fill with tears
*“His eyes were watering”*

<a id="entry-w-000363"></a>

*n.* 1. **紫萼路邊青** — erect perennial of north temperate zone having pinnate leaves and a few nodding flowers with a brown-purple calyx and orange and pink petals

*n.* 1. *(historical)* **滴漏, 金壺 /金壺, 刻漏, 漏壺 /漏壺, 漏刻, 水漏, 銅壺 /銅壺, 懸泉 /懸泉, 水鐘 /水鍾** — A device for measuring time using water, typically by measuring water flowing in or out of a basin of known size at a known rate.

<a id="entry-w-000522"></a>
## wave

**發音：** /weɪv/
*n.* 1. **波浪, 波** — one of a series of ridges that moves across the surface of a liquid (especially across a large body of water)
2. **揮手示意, 揮手致意** — a movement like that of a sudden occurrence or increase in a specified phenomenon
*“a wave of settlers”*
*“troops advancing in waves”*
3. **波浪, 波, 浪** — A moving disturbance in the level of a body of liquid; an undulation.
4. **波** — A moving disturbance in the energy level of a field.
5. **波形, 波浪形, 波狀輪廓** — an undulating curve
6. (physics) a movement up and down or back and forth
*v.* 1. **招手, 揮手示意, 點頭示意** — signal with the hands or nod
*“She waved to her friends”*
*“He waved his hand hospitably”*
2. *(intransitive)* **招手, 揮手 /揮手** — To move one's hand back and forth (generally above the shoulders) in greeting or departure.
3. move or swing back and forth
*“She waved her gun”*
4. move in a wavy pattern or with a rising and falling motion
*“The curtains undulated”*
*“the waves rolled towards the beach”*
5. twist or roll into coils or ringlets
*“curl my hair, please”*
6. set waves in
*“she asked the hairdresser to wave her hair”*

*v.* 1. **舉白旗 /舉白旗** — To indicate to an opposing force that one is surrendering.

<a id="entry-w-000525"></a>
## wave-particle duality

2. (physics) the property of matter and electromagnetic radiation that is characterized by the fact that some properties can be explained best by wave theory and others by particle theory

## waveband

2. a band of adjacent radio frequencies (e.g., assigned for transmitting radio or television signals)

<a id="entry-w-000527"></a>
## waveform

*n.* 1. **波形** — The shape of a wave function represented by a graph showing some dependent variable as function of an independent variable.
2. the shape of a wave illustrated graphically by plotting the values of the period quantity against time

<a id="entry-w-000528"></a>
## wavefunction

**發音：** /ˈweɪv.fʌŋ(k).ʃən/
*n.* 1. **波函數 /波函數** — A mathematical function that describes the propagation of the quantum mechanical wave associated with a particle (or system of particles), related to the probability of finding the particle in a particular region of space.

<a id="entry-w-000529"></a>
## wavefunction collapse

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **波函數坍縮 /波函數坍縮** — The phenomenon in which a wavefunction — initially in a superposition of several different possible eigenstates — appears to reduce to a single one of those states after interaction with an observer: the reduction of the physical possibilities into a single possibility as seen by an observer.

<a id="entry-w-000530"></a>
## waveguide

*n.* 1. **波導 /波導** — A structure which guides waves, such as electromagnetic waves, light, or sound waves.
2. a hollow metal conductor that provides a path to guide microwaves; used in radar

<a id="entry-w-000531"></a>
## wavelength

**發音：** /ˈweɪvlɛŋ(k)θ/
*n.* 1. **波長 /波長** — The length of a single cycle of a wave, as measured by the distance between one peak or trough of a wave and the next; it is often designated in physics as λ, and corresponds to the velocity of the wave divided by its frequency.
2. the distance (measured in the direction of propagation) between two points in the same phase in consecutive cycles of a wave
3. a shared orientation leading to mutual understanding
*“they are on the same wavelength”*

<a id="entry-w-000532"></a>
## wavelet

*n.* 1. **小浪** — A small wave; a ripple.
2. **小波** — A fast-decaying oscillation.
3. a small wave on the surface of a liquid

<a id="entry-w-000534"></a>
## waver

**發音：** /ˈweɪvə/

*“Authorities hesitate to quote exact figures”*
2. **動搖, 變弱, 變得軟弱無力, 變衰弱, 搖擺** — be unsure or weak
*“Their enthusiasm is faltering”*
3. *(figuratively, intransitive)* **動搖 /動搖** — To begin to weaken or show signs of weakening in resolve; to falter, to flinch, to give way.
4. move hesitatingly, as if about to give way
5. move or sway in a rising and falling or wavelike pattern
*“the line on the monitor vacillated”*
6. move back and forth very rapidly
*“the candle flickered”*
*n.* 1. **搖曳, 擺動, 搖擺** — the act of moving back and forth
2. someone who communicates by waving
3. the act of pausing uncertainly
*“there was a hesitation in his speech”*

*“he kept a record of price fluctuations”*

*adj.* 1. **猶豫+的, 優柔寡斷+的, 猶豫不決+的, 躊躇+的** — uncertain in purpose or action

**發音：** /waks/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **蠟 /蠟, 蜜蠟 /蜜蠟** — Any oily, water-resistant, solid or semisolid substance; normally long-chain hydrocarbons, alcohols or esters.
2. any of various substances of either mineral origin or plant or animal origin; they are solid at normal temperatures and insoluble in water
*v.* 1. **上漲, 升起, 增加, 攀升, 上升** — go up or advance
*“Sales were climbing after prices were lowered”*
2. *(transitive)* **蜜蠟脫毛 /蜜蠟脫毛, 熱蠟脫毛 /熱蠟脫毛** — To remove hair at the roots from (a part of the body) by coating the skin with a film of wax that is then pulled away sharply.

**發音：** /wɛə/
*v.* 1. **戴, 穿** — be dressed in

*“He always wears a smile”*

*“This dress wore well for almost ten years”*

*“The gears wore out”*
6. have on one's person

*“bear a scar”*

*n.* 1. **侵蝕, 腐蝕** — (geology) the mechanical process of wearing or grinding something down (as by particles washing over it)
2. the act of having on your person as a covering or adornment
*“she bought it for everyday wear”*
*adj.* 1. producing exhaustion
*“an exhausting march”*
*“the visit was especially wearing”*

*“she was refined in her choice of apparel”*
*“he always bought his clothes at the same store”*

<a id="entry-w-000615"></a>
## wearing away

*n.* 1. **侵蝕, 腐蝕** — (geology) the mechanical process of wearing or grinding something down (as by particles washing over it)

## weary

*v.* 1. exhaust or get tired through overuse or great strain or stress
*“We wore ourselves out on this hike”*
2. lose interest or become bored with something or somebody
*“I'm so tired of your mother and her complaints about my food”*

3. a person who is regarded as treacherous or sneaky

2. an equivocal qualification; a word used to avoid making an outright assertion

<a id="entry-w-000619"></a>
## weather

**發音：** /ˈwɛðə/
*n.* 1. **天氣, 天氣條件** — the atmospheric conditions that comprise the state of the atmosphere in terms of temperature and wind and clouds and precipitation
*“they were hoping for good weather”*
*“every day we have weather conditions and yesterday was no exception”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **天氣 /天氣, 氣候 /氣候, 天景** — The short-term state of the atmosphere at a specific time and place, including the temperature, relative humidity, cloud cover, precipitation, wind, etc.
*adj.* 1. towards the side exposed to wind
*v.* 1. **勇敢地面對, 經受住, 面對, 忍耐, 忍受** — face and withstand with courage
*“She braved the elements”*
2. cause to slope
3. sail to the windward of
4. change under the action or influence of the weather
*“A weathered old hut”*

<a id="entry-w-000620"></a>
## weather condition

*n.* 1. **天氣, 天氣條件** — the atmospheric conditions that comprise the state of the atmosphere in terms of temperature and wind and clouds and precipitation
*“they were hoping for good weather”*
*“every day we have weather conditions and yesterday was no exception”*

2. a forecast of the weather

*n.* 1. **氣象報告員 /氣象報告員** — A person who forecasts the weather.
2. predicts the weather

<a id="entry-w-000640"></a>
## web

**發音：** /wɛb/
*n.* 1. *(broadly)* **網 /網** — Any interconnected set of persons, places, or things, which, when diagrammed, resembles a spider's web.
2. **網絡** — an interconnected system of things or people
*“he owned a network of shops”*

*“the trees cast a delicate web of shadows over the lawn”*

5. the flattened weblike part of a feather consisting of a series of barbs on either side of the shaft

*adj.* 1. **有雜草+的, 雜草叢生+的, 雜草多+的** — abounding with or resembling weeds
*“a weedy path”*
*“weedy plants that take over a garden”*
2. being very thin
*“a child with skinny freckled legs”*
*“a long scrawny neck”*

<a id="entry-w-000696"></a>
## week

*n.* 1. **一個星期, 一週, 一星期, 七天, 星期** — any period of seven consecutive days
*“it rained for a week”*
2. **周, 星期, 禮拜** — a period of seven consecutive days starting on Sunday
3. hours or days of work in a calendar week
*“they worked a 40-hour week”*

<a id="entry-w-000697"></a>

*“a week from Monday will be too soon!”*

<a id="entry-w-000698"></a>

*n.* 1. **工作日, 平常日, 平日** — any day except Sunday (and sometimes except Saturday)
2. **平日, 平常日** — Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday or Friday, but not Saturday or Sunday.

<a id="entry-w-000699"></a>
## weekend

**發音：** /wiːˈkɛnd/
*n.* 1. **週末** — a time period usually extending from Friday night through Sunday; more loosely defined as any period of successive days including one and only one Sunday
2. **週末 /週末** — The break in the working week, usually two days including the traditional holy or sabbath day. Thus in Western countries, Saturday and Sunday.
*v.* 1. **度週末, 過週末** — spend the weekend

<a id="entry-w-000700"></a>
## weekly

**發音：** /ˈwiːk.li/
*n.* 1. **週刊** — A publication that is published once a week.
2. a periodical that is published every week (or 52 issues per year)
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **每週 /每週** — Of or relating to a week.
2. **一週+的, 一週一次+的, 每七天+的, 每週** — of or occurring every seven days
*“a weekly visit”*
*“weekly paper”*
3. *(not-comparable)* **每週 /每週** — Happening once a week, or every week.
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **每週 /每週** — Once every week.
2. *(not-comparable)* **每週 /每週** — Every week.
3. without missing a week
*“she visited her aunt weekly”*

*“The butcher weighed the chicken”*

*“It does not matter much”*

5. **重壓** — to be oppressive or burdensome
*“weigh heavily on the mind", "Something pressed on his mind”*

<a id="entry-w-000722"></a>
## weight

**發音：** /weɪt/
*n.* 1. **重量, 重力** — the vertical force exerted by a mass as a result of gravity
2. **槓鈴片** — sports equipment used in calisthenic exercises and weightlifting; it is not attached to anything and is raised and lowered by use of the hands and arms
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **重量, 體重 /體重** — The downwards force an object experiences due to gravity.
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **配重** — An object used to make something heavier.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **砝碼 /砝碼** — A standardized block of metal used in a balance to measure the mass of another object.
6. **權重系統** — a system of units used to express the weight of something
*v.* 1. weight down with a load
2. present with a bias
*“He biased his presentation so as to please the share holders”*

*“she lives on welfare”*

<a id="entry-w-000765"></a>
## well

*adv.* 1. (often used as a combining form) in a good or proper or satisfactory manner or to a high standard (`good' is a nonstandard dialectal variant for `well')
*“the children behaved well”*
*“a task well done”*
2. thoroughly or completely; fully; often used as a combining form
*“The problem is well understood”*
*“she was well informed”*
3. indicating high probability; in all likelihood
*“I might well do it”*
*“a mistake that could easily have ended in disaster”*
4. (used for emphasis or as an intensifier) entirely or fully
*“a book well worth reading”*
*“was well aware of the difficulties ahead”*
5. to a suitable or appropriate extent or degree
*“the project was well underway”*
*“the fetus has well developed organs”*
6. favorably; with approval
*“their neighbors spoke well of them”*
*“he thought well of the book”*
*n.* 1. **井, 水井** — a deep hole or shaft dug or drilled to obtain water or oil or gas or brine
2. a cavity or vessel used to contain liquid
3. an abundant source
*“she was a well of information”*
4. an open shaft through the floors of a building (as for a stairway)
5. an enclosed compartment in a ship or plane for holding something as e.g. fish or a plane's landing gear or for protecting something as e.g. a ship's pumps
*adj.* 1. **健康+的** — in good health especially after having suffered illness or injury
*“appears to be entirely well”*
*“the wound is nearly well”*
2. resulting favorably
*“it's a good thing that I wasn't there”*
*“it is good that you stayed”*
3. wise or advantageous and hence advisable
*“it would be well to start early”*
*v.* 1. **湧出** — come up, as of a liquid
*“Tears well in her eyes”*
*“the currents well up”*

*n.* 1. **鯨鬚, 鯨骨** — a horny material from the upper jaws of certain whales; used as the ribs of fans or as stays in corsets

<a id="entry-w-000960"></a>
## whalebone whale

*n.* 1. **鬚鯨** — whale with plates of whalebone along the upper jaw for filtering plankton from the water

<a id="entry-w-000961"></a>
## whaling

**發音：** /ˈweɪlɪŋ/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **捕鯨 /捕鯨** — The practice of hunting whales.

<a id="entry-w-000962"></a>
## whaling station

*n.* 1. **鯨魚加工場 /鯨魚加工場, 捕鯨站 /捕鯨站** — A dock in which the carcasses of hunted whales are processed, and where whale hunters sometimes live.

<a id="entry-w-000963"></a>
## wharf

**發音：** /ˈwɔːf/
*n.* 1. **碼頭 /碼頭, 港埠, 埠頭 /埠頭** — An artificial landing place for ships on a riverbank or shore.
2. a platform built out from the shore into the water and supported by piles; provides access to ships and boats
*v.* 1. **入塢, 卸上碼頭** — store on a wharf
*“Wharf the merchandise”*
2. provide with a wharf
*“Wharf the mouth of the river”*
3. discharge at a wharf
*“wharf the passengers”*
4. come into or dock at a wharf
*“the big ship wharfed in the evening”*
5. moor at a wharf
*“The ship was wharfed”*

<a id="entry-w-000964"></a>
## wharf rat

*n.* 1. **碼頭鼠** — brown rat that infests wharves
2. someone who lives near wharves and lives by pilfering from ships or warehouses

<a id="entry-w-000965"></a>
## wharfage

*n.* 1. **碼頭使用費, 碼頭費, 停泊費** — a fee charged for the use of a wharf or quay
2. a platform built out from the shore into the water and supported by piles; provides access to ships and boats

<a id="entry-w-000966"></a>
## what a lovely day

*phr.* 1. **真是美好的一天** — The day is lovely.

<a id="entry-w-000967"></a>
## what a pity

**發音：** /ˌwʌt ə ˈpɪti/ [ˌwɐɾ ə ˈpʰɪɾi ~ ˌwɜɾ ə ˈpʰɪɾi]
*phr.* 1. **真遺憾 /真遺憾, 真倒黴** — Used to express regret or disappointment about an unfortunate event or piece of information.

<a id="entry-w-000968"></a>
## what about

*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **...怎麼樣 /...怎麼樣** — Used to make a suggestion.
2. *(not-comparable)* **...怎麼樣 /...怎麼樣, ...呢** — Used to ask someone to consider something or someone that they have apparently not considered.

<a id="entry-w-000969"></a>
## what are friends for

*interj.* 1. **這是朋友該做的** — Said by one friend to another in response to thank you.

<a id="entry-w-000970"></a>
## what are you doing

*phr.* 1. **你在幹什麼？ /你在幹什麼？, 你幹嘛呢？ /你幹嘛呢？** — Used to ask what the interlocutor is currently doing.

<a id="entry-w-000971"></a>
## what date is it today

*phr.* 1. **今天幾號？ /今天幾號？** — What is the date? (used to request the current date)

<a id="entry-w-000972"></a>
## what day is it today

*phr.* 1. **今天星期幾？ /今天星期幾？, 今天幾號？ /今天幾號？** — Used to ask for the current day of the week or of the month.

<a id="entry-w-000973"></a>
## what do you mean

*phr.* 1. **你這是什麼意思？ /你這是什麼意思？** — A request for clarification of an anterior statement or question.

<a id="entry-w-000974"></a>
## what do you say

*phr.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **你覺得怎麼樣？ /你覺得怎麼樣？, 好嗎？ /好嗎？, 你說呢？ /你說呢？** — Used to ask someone if they are willing to do something.
2. *(idiomatic)* **說謝謝 /說謝謝** — Used to remind a child to say a polite expression.

<a id="entry-w-000975"></a>
## what does that have to do with the price of tea in China

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial, rhetoric)* **八竿子打不著, 八竿子打不著, 風馬牛不相及 /風馬牛不相及, 驢唇不對馬嘴 /驢唇不對馬嘴** — Used to indicate that a previous comment is irrelevant or non sequitur.

<a id="entry-w-000976"></a>
## what does … mean

*phr.* 1. **……是什麼意思？ /……是什麼意思？** — Used to ask the meaning of a word (replace “…” with a word).

<a id="entry-w-000977"></a>
## what doesn't kill you makes you stronger

*phr.* 1. **艱難困苦，玉汝於成 /艱難困苦, 玉汝於成, 逆境造就品格** — Hardship builds moral character.

<a id="entry-w-000978"></a>
## what for

*n.* 1. a strong reprimand
*adv.* 1. *(colloquial, not-comparable)* **為什麼 /為什麼** — For what reason; why.

<a id="entry-w-000979"></a>
## what goes around comes around

*phr.* 1. **善有善報，惡有惡報 /善有善報, 惡有惡報, 種瓜得瓜 /種瓜得瓜, 種豆得豆 /種豆得豆, 媳婦熬成婆 /媳婦熬成婆, 因果循環，報應不爽。** — A person's actions, whether good or bad, will often have consequences for that person.

<a id="entry-w-000980"></a>
## what have you

*pron.* 1. *(colloquial)* **之類的 /之類的, 什麼的 /什麼的, 等等** — Any of several additional, unmentioned things.

<a id="entry-w-000981"></a>
## what if

*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **不如, 萬一 /萬一** — Used to introduce a suggestion or proposal for a future event.

<a id="entry-w-000982"></a>
## what is her name

*phr.* 1. **她叫什麼名字？ /她叫什麼名字？** — Used to ask the name of a woman.

<a id="entry-w-000983"></a>
## what is his name

*phr.* 1. **他叫什麼名字？ /他叫什麼名字？** — Used to ask the name of a man.

<a id="entry-w-000984"></a>
## what is more

*adv.* 1. *(idiomatic, not-comparable)* **並且 /並且, 而且, 還有 /還有, 況且 /況且** — Used to introduce a statement which builds on the preceding statement, but expresses a stronger sentiment.
2. in addition
*“computer chess games are getting cheaper all the time; furthermore, their quality is improving"”*
*“the cellar was dark; moreover, mice nested there"”*

<a id="entry-w-000985"></a>
## what is that

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial)* **那是什麼？ /那是什麼？, 這是什麼？ /這是什麼？** — What did you just say? Synonym of say again.

<a id="entry-w-000986"></a>
## what is the Wi-Fi password

*phr.* 1. **無線網絡的密碼是多少？ /無線網絡的密碼是多少？, 無線網路的密碼是多少？ /無線網路的密碼是多少？** — Asks someone for the Wi-Fi password.

<a id="entry-w-000987"></a>
## what is your name

*phr.* 1. **你叫什麼名字？ /你叫什麼名字？, 你怎麼稱呼？ /你怎麼稱呼？, 您貴姓？ /您貴姓？** — Used to ask the name of a person.

<a id="entry-w-000988"></a>
## what kind of

*det.* 1. *(no-gloss, translation-hub)* **哪種 /哪種**

<a id="entry-w-000989"></a>
## what kind of music do you like

*phr.* 1. **你喜歡哪種音樂？ /你喜歡哪種音樂？** — What kind of music do you like?

<a id="entry-w-000990"></a>
## what languages do you speak

*phr.* 1. **你會說哪些語言？ /你會說哪些語言？** — What are the languages which you can understand and which you can speak or write in?

<a id="entry-w-000991"></a>
## what nonsense

*phr.* 1. **胡說八道 /胡說八道, 胡說八道** — That is nonsense; that is patently untrue.

<a id="entry-w-000992"></a>
## what number

*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **第幾 /第幾** — Which, what position (in an ordered series, such as which day of the month or which floor). (This entry is a translation hub.)

<a id="entry-w-000993"></a>
## what should I do

*phr.* 1. **我該怎麼辦？ /我該怎麼辦？** — what should I do?

<a id="entry-w-000994"></a>
## what the fuck

*interj.* 1. *(vulgar)* **什麼鳥？ /什麼鳥？, 什麼鬼 /什麼鬼** — Used to express astonishment, shock, incredulity, or disbelief (as an ellipsis of longer expressions).
*phr.* 1. *(interrogative, slang, vulgar)* **什麼鳥？ /什麼鳥？, 什麼鬼 /什麼鬼** — An intensive form of what.

<a id="entry-w-000995"></a>
## what the hell

*phr.* 1. *(mildly, slang, vulgar)* **什麼鬼 /什麼鬼** — An intensive form of what.

<a id="entry-w-000996"></a>
## what time is it

*phr.* 1. **幾點了？ /幾點了？** — What is the time of day? (used to request for the current hour and approximate number of minutes after the hour)

<a id="entry-w-000997"></a>
## what was that

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial)* **你剛才說什麼？ /你剛才說什麼？** — What did you just say? Synonym of say again.

<a id="entry-w-000998"></a>
## what would you like

*phr.* 1. **你想要什麼？ /你想要什麼？** — Used to ask the interlocutor what they would like to order, to buy or have a look at.

<a id="entry-w-000999"></a>
## what you see is what you get

*phr.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **所見即所得 /所見即所得** — The screen image resembles the printed output.

<a id="entry-w-001000"></a>
## what's done is done

*phr.* 1. **覆水難收 /覆水難收, 木已成舟** — Events that have already taken place cannot be changed and actions that have already been committed cannot be undone, so it is best not to dwell on them.

<a id="entry-w-001001"></a>
## what's going on

*interj.* 1. *(idiomatic, informal)* **在幹嘛呢 /在幹嘛呢** — An informal greeting.
2. *(idiomatic, informal)* **怎麼了 /怎麼了** — An expression of concern, asking for an explanation.

<a id="entry-w-001002"></a>
## what's good

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial)* **這裡有什麼好吃的？ /這裡有什麼好吃的？** — What is good, especially in reference to food.

<a id="entry-w-001003"></a>
## what's it to you

*phr.* 1. **關...什麼事 /關...什麼事, 關...屁事 /關...屁事, 不關...的事 /不關...的事** — Why are you asking? Why do you want to know?

<a id="entry-w-001004"></a>
## what's new

*phr.* 1. *(informal)* **最近怎麼樣？ /最近怎麼樣？** — An informal greeting asking the other person what has recently happened in their life. A typical response might be "Not much; you?". The greeting is not always literal and may just be used to say hello.

<a id="entry-w-001005"></a>
## what's on your mind

*phr.* 1. **你在想什麼？ /你在想什麼？, 你怎麼了？ /你怎麼了？** — What are you thinking about?

<a id="entry-w-001006"></a>
## what's that

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial)* **那是什麼？ /那是什麼？** — What is that?
2. *(colloquial)* **那是什麼？ /那是什麼？** — What was that? Synonym of say again.

<a id="entry-w-001007"></a>
## what's the good of

*phr.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **……有什麼用？ /……有什麼用？** — What is the purpose or advantage of

<a id="entry-w-001008"></a>
## what's the matter

*phr.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **什麼事？ /什麼事？, 怎麼了？ /怎麼了？** — What is wrong? What's the problem?

<a id="entry-w-001009"></a>
## what's up

*phr.* 1. *(US, colloquial, idiomatic)* **怎麼樣 /怎麼樣** — What's happening? What are you doing?

<a id="entry-w-001010"></a>
## what's your job

*phr.* 1. **你的工作是什麼? /你的工作是什麼?** — Inquires about the subject's current occupation.

<a id="entry-w-001011"></a>
## what's your phone number

*phr.* 1. **你的電話號碼是多少？ /你的電話號碼是多少？** — Asks the interlocutor to give their phone number for subsequent communication.

<a id="entry-w-001012"></a>
## what's your poison

**發音：** /ˌwɒts jə ˈpɔɪz(ə)n/
*phr.* 1. *(dated, humorous, idiomatic)* **你想喝什麼酒 /你想喝什麼酒** — Used to someone what alcoholic beverage they would like to drink: what drink would you like to have?

<a id="entry-w-001013"></a>
## whataboutism

**發音：** /ˌwə.təˈbaʊ.tɪ.zəm/
*n.* 1. *(countable, rhetoric, uncountable)* **比爛主義 /比爛主義, 臭蟲論 /臭蟲論** — A logical fallacy where criticisms are deflected by raising unrelated criticisms of the opposite side.

<a id="entry-w-001014"></a>
## whatchamacallit

**發音：** /ˈwɒtʃəməˌkɔːlɪt/
*n.* 1. *(colloquial)* **那個那個, 那個東西, 東東 /東東** — A metasyntactic term used for any object whose actual name the speaker does not know or cannot remember; a doodad, gizmo, thingamajig, thingy.
2. **某人, 某人或某物, 那個叫啥的** — something unspecified whose name is either forgotten or not known
*“she eased the ball-shaped doodad back into its socket”*
*“there may be some great new gizmo around the corner that you will want to use”*

<a id="entry-w-001015"></a>
## whatchamacallum

*n.* 1. **某人, 某人或某物, 那個叫啥的** — something unspecified whose name is either forgotten or not known
*“she eased the ball-shaped doodad back into its socket”*
*“there may be some great new gizmo around the corner that you will want to use”*

<a id="entry-w-001016"></a>
## whatever

**發音：** /ʍɒtˈɛv.ə/
*adj.* 1. **不管什麼樣+的, 任何, 無論什麼樣+的** — one or some or every or all without specification
*“give me any peaches you don't want”*
*“not any milk is left”*
*pron.* 1. *(interrogative)* **不管什麼 /不管什麼, 隨便 /隨便** — What ever; emphasised form of 'what', used to ask which thing, event, circumstance, etc.
*det.* 1. **不管怎樣 /不管怎樣** — Regardless of the ... that; for any ... that.
*interj.* 1. *(colloquial)* **隨便 /隨便, 好吧** — A holophrastic expression used to dismiss something that the speaker doesn't care about, doesn't think important, or doesn't want to consider or discuss any further. Sometimes conceived as an ellipsis of whatever you say, implying feigned deference: whatever you say [apparently must be true].

<a id="entry-w-001017"></a>
## whatever the case may be

*phr.* 1. **不管是什麼情況 /不管是什麼情況** — As is appropriate to the circumstances; particularly where a choice must be made between two options.

<a id="entry-w-001018"></a>
## whatnot

*n.* 1. **小古玩, 小古董, 小裝飾品類, 放古董的架子** — miscellaneous curios

## WhatsApp

*prop. n.* 1. **瓦次艾普, 瓦次普** — An instant messaging service founded in 2009 and acquired by Facebook in 2014.

<a id="entry-w-001020"></a>
## whatsis

*n.* 1. **某人, 某人或某物, 那個叫啥的** — something unspecified whose name is either forgotten or not known
*“she eased the ball-shaped doodad back into its socket”*
*“there may be some great new gizmo around the corner that you will want to use”*

<a id="entry-w-001021"></a>
## whatsoever

**發音：** /ˌwɒtsəʊˈɛvə(ɹ)/
*adj.* 1. **不管什麼樣+的, 任何, 無論什麼樣+的** — one or some or every or all without specification
*“give me any peaches you don't want”*
*“not any milk is left”*
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **任何, 什麼...也... /什麼...也..., 什麼...都... /什麼...都...** — In any way; at all; whatever.

## wheat

4. a variable yellow tint; dull yellow, often diluted with white

*“wheaten bread”*

<a id="entry-w-001032"></a>
## wheatgrass

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **小麥草 /小麥草** — Young grass of the common wheat plant, Triticum aestivum, used freshly juiced or dried into powder for animal and human consumption.
2. *(uncountable, usually)* **小麥草 /小麥草** — A grass of the genus Agropyron, commonly known as crested-wheat grass.
3. a grass of the genus Agropyron

<a id="entry-w-001035"></a>
## wheedle

**發音：** /ˈʍiː.dəl/
*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **誘哄 /誘哄** — To cajole or attempt to persuade by flattery.
2. influence or urge by gentle urging, caressing, or flattering
*“He palavered her into going along”*

<a id="entry-w-001036"></a>
## wheel

*n.* 1. **輪, 輪子** — a simple machine consisting of a circular frame with spokes (or a solid disc) that can rotate on a shaft or axle (as in vehicles or other machines)
2. **單車, 腳踏車, 自行車** — a wheeled vehicle that has two wheels and is moved by foot pedals
3. a handwheel that is used for steering
4. forces that provide energy and direction
*“the wheels of government began to turn”*
5. a circular helm to control the rudder of a vessel
6. game equipment consisting of a wheel with slots that is used for gambling; the wheel rotates horizontally and players bet on which slot the roulette ball will stop in
*v.* 1. **轉彎** — change directions as if revolving on a pivot
*“They wheeled their horses around and left”*
2. **騎自行車, 騎車** — ride a bicycle
3. wheel somebody or something
4. move along on or as if on wheels or a wheeled vehicle
*“The President's convoy rolled past the crowds”*

<a id="entry-w-001037"></a>
## wheel and axle

*n.* 1. **輪軸 /輪軸** — A simple machine consisting of a wheel (or similar lever) that turns an axle around which a cord is wound; its mechanical advantage is the ratio of the diameters of the wheel and axle.
2. hoist so arranged that a rope unwinding from a wheel is wound onto a cylindrical drum or shaft coaxial with the wheel

<a id="entry-w-001038"></a>
## wheel around

*v.* 1. **轉彎** — change directions as if revolving on a pivot
*“They wheeled their horses around and left”*
2. wheel somebody or something

<a id="entry-w-001039"></a>
## wheel bug

*n.* 1. **齒輪蟲** — large predatory North American bug that sucks the blood of other insects

<a id="entry-w-001040"></a>
## wheel horse

*n.* 1. **轅馬** — a draft horse harnessed behind others and nearest the wheels of a vehicle

<a id="entry-w-001041"></a>
## Wheel of Fortune

**發音：** /ˌwiːl‿əv ˈfɔːt͡ʃuːn/
*prop. n.* 1. *(broadly)* **命運之輪 /命運之輪** — A tarot card with an image of Fortuna's wheel (sense 1), generally the tenth of 22 trumps of the major arcana in most tarot decks.

<a id="entry-w-001042"></a>
## wheelbarrow

**發音：** /ˈwiːlbæɹəʊ/
*n.* 1. **獨輪車 /獨輪車, 手推車 /手推車, 轆轤車 /轆轤車** — A small, one-wheeled (or rarely, two-wheeled) cart with handles at one end for transporting small loads.
2. **手推車, 獨輪車** — a cart for carrying small loads; has handles and one or more wheels
*v.* 1. transport in a wheelbarrow

<a id="entry-w-001043"></a>
## wheelchair

**發音：** /ˈwiːlt͡ʃɛə(ɹ)/
*n.* 1. **輪椅 /輪椅** — A chair mounted on large wheels for the transportation or use of a sick or disabled person.
2. a movable chair mounted on large wheels; for invalids or those who cannot walk; frequently propelled by the occupant

<a id="entry-w-001044"></a>
## wheeled

*adj.* 1. **有輪+的, 有輪子+的** — having wheels; often used in combination

<a id="entry-w-001045"></a>
## wheeler

<a id="entry-w-001132"></a>
## whistle

**發音：** /ˈwɪs(ə)l/
*v.* 1. **發噓噓聲, 吹口哨** — make whistling sounds
*“He lay there, snoring and whistling”*
2. **呼嘯而過, 嗖嗖飛過** — move with, or as with, a whistling sound
*“The bullets whistled past him”*
3. *(ambitransitive)* **吹口哨, 鳴笛 /鳴笛** — To make a shrill, high-pitched sound by forcing air through the mouth. To produce a whistling sound, restrictions to the flow of air are created using the teeth, tongue and lips.
4. utter or express by whistling
*“She whistled a melody”*
5. move, send, or bring as if by whistling
*“Her optimism whistled away these worries”*
6. make a whining, ringing, or whistling sound
*“the kettle was singing”*
*“the bullet sang past his ear”*
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **哨子, 口哨, 口笛, 汽笛** — A device designed to be placed in the mouth and blown, or driven by steam or some other mechanism, to make a whistling sound.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **口哨, 口笛** — An act of whistling.
3. the sound made by something moving rapidly or by steam coming out of a small aperture
4. the act of signalling (e.g., summoning) by whistling or blowing a whistle
*“the whistle signalled the end of the game”*
5. a small wind instrument that produces a whistling sound by blowing into it
6. acoustic device that forces air or steam against an edge or into a cavity and so produces a loud shrill sound

<a id="entry-w-001133"></a>
## whistle Dixie

*v.* 1. *(Southern-US, idiomatic)* **想入非非** — To engage in idle conversational fantasies.

<a id="entry-w-001134"></a>

<a id="entry-w-001135"></a>

## white

*adj.* 1. **白, 白色+的** — being of the achromatic color of maximum lightness; having little or no hue owing to reflection of almost all incident light
*“as white as fresh snow”*
*“a bride's white dress”*
2. **白種+的, 白種人+的** — of or belonging to a racial group having light skin coloration
*“voting patterns within the white population”*
3. **空白+的, 乾淨+的, 清白+的** — (of a surface) not written or printed on
*“blank pages”*
*“fill in the blank spaces”*
4. **無血色+的, 沒有精神+的, 鐵青+的, 蒼白+的** — anemic looking from illness or emotion
*“a face turned ashen”*
*“the invalid's blanched cheeks”*
5. free from moral blemish or impurity; unsullied
*“in shining white armor”*
6. marked by the presence of snow
*“a white Christmas”*
*“the white hills of a northern winter”*
*n.* 1. **褲子** — (usually in the plural) trousers made of flannel or gabardine or tweed or white cloth
2. a member of the Caucasoid race
3. the quality or state of the achromatic color of greatest lightness (bearing the least resemblance to black)
4. United States jurist appointed chief justice of the United States Supreme Court in 1910 by President Taft; noted for his work on antitrust legislation (1845-1921)
5. Australian writer (1912-1990)
6. United States political journalist (1915-1986)
*v.* 1. turn white
*“This detergent will whiten your laundry”*

## whitewash

**發音：** /ˈwaɪtwɒʃ/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **大白, 膩子 /膩子** — A mixture of a powdered mineral substance (often slaked lime (containing calcium hydroxide), chalk (calcium carbonate), or both) and water which is used for painting surfaces such as fences and walls bright white.
2. a defeat in which the losing person or team fails to score
3. wash consisting of lime and size in water; used for whitening walls and other surfaces
4. a specious or deceptive clearing that attempts to gloss over failings and defects
*v.* 1. **隱瞞, 掩蓋, 遮掩** — cover up a misdemeanor, fault, or error
*“Let's not whitewash the crimes of Stalin”*
*“She tried to gloss over her mistakes”*
2. *(figuratively, often, transitive)* **洗白** — To hide (someone's) mistakes, or to create an appearance of honesty, propriety, etc., for (someone); also, to intentionally hide or overlook (mistakes, unfavourable facts, wrongdoing, etc.).
3. cover with whitewash
*“whitewash walls”*
4. exonerate by means of a perfunctory investigation or through biased presentation of data

**發音：** /ˈdʌbəljuː ˈeɪt͡ʃ ˈəʊ/
*prop. n.* 1. *(abbreviation, alt-of, initialism)* **WHO, 世衛組織 /世衛組織, 世衛 /世衛** — Initialism of World Health Organization.

<a id="entry-w-001308"></a>
## who are you

*phr.* 1. **你是誰？ /你是誰？, 您是哪位？** — Asks the interlocutor for their identification

<a id="entry-w-001309"></a>
## who cares

*phr.* 1. *(colloquial, rhetoric)* **誰在乎 /誰在乎, 誰關心啊 /誰關心啊** — A reply to an unimportant or irrelevant statement, indicating indifference on the part of the speaker.

<a id="entry-w-001310"></a>
## who keeps company with the wolf will learn to howl

*phr.* 1. **近朱者赤，近墨者黑** — A person tends to acquire the characteristics of those with whom they associate.

<a id="entry-w-001311"></a>
## who knows

*phr.* 1. **誰知道？ /誰知道？** — A rhetorical question asked to express the idea that anything is possible or that anything could happen.

<a id="entry-w-001312"></a>
## who's calling

*phr.* 1. **你是誰？ /你是誰？, 您是哪位？** — Used by a phone call receiver to ask the identity of the caller.

<a id="entry-w-001313"></a>
## whoa

**發音：** /wəʊ/
*interj.* 1. **籲** — Stop (especially when commanding a horse or imitative thereof); calm down; slow down.

<a id="entry-w-001314"></a>
## whodunit

**發音：** /ˌhuːˈdʌn.ɪt/
*n.* 1. **偵探小說 /偵探小說, 犯罪小說 /犯罪小說** — A novel or drama concerning a crime (usually a murder) in which a detective follows clues to determine the perpetrator.
2. a story about a crime (usually murder) presented as a novel or play or movie

<a id="entry-w-001315"></a>
## whole

**發音：** /həʊl/
*adj.* 1. **所有+的, 完整+的** — including all components without exception; being one unit or constituting the full amount or extent or duration; complete
*“gave his whole attention”*
*“a whole wardrobe for the tropics”*
2. **整個 /整個, 全部** — Entire, undivided.
3. **全體一致的, 一致+的** — acting together as a single undiversified whole
*“a solid voting bloc”*
4. (of siblings) having the same parents
*“whole brothers and sisters”*
5. not injured
6. exhibiting or restored to vigorous good health
*“hale and hearty”*
*“whole in mind and body”*
*n.* 1. **部件, 單位** — an assemblage of parts that is regarded as a single entity
*“how big is that part compared to the whole?”*
*“the team is a unit”*
2. all of something including all its component elements or parts
*“Europe considered as a whole”*
*“the whole of American literature”*
*adv.* 1. **全, 全體+地, 全然+地, 全部+的, 十分+地** — to a complete degree or to the full or entire extent (`whole' is often used informally for `wholly')
*“he was wholly convinced”*
*“entirely satisfied with the meal”*

*“a hotdog with the works”*

**發音：** /ˌhəʊlˈhɑː(ɹ)t.əd/

2. **全心全意+的, 一心一意+的, 真誠+的** — with unconditional and enthusiastic devotion
*“heart-whole friendship”*
*“gave wholehearted support to her candidacy”*

**發音：** /ˌhəʊlˈhɑːtɪdli/
*adv.* 1. **殫心 /殫心, 竭誠 /竭誠, 經心 /經心, 盡心 /盡心, 念念, 全心全意, 悉心, 一門心思 /一門心思, 一心, 一心一意** — In a wholehearted (“showing enthusiastic and unconditional commitment”) manner; without reserve; enthusiastically, unreservedly.

*“he adopted wholeheartedly some of the policies that he had previously criticized”*

## wholeness

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **完整性** — The quality of being whole.
2. **完整, 完全** — an undivided or unbroken completeness or totality with nothing wanting
*“the integrity of the nervous system is required for normal development”*
*“he took measures to insure the territorial unity of Croatia”*
3. **健壯** — a state of robust good health

<a id="entry-w-001337"></a>
## wholesale

**發音：** /ˈhoʊlseɪl/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **批發 /批發** — The sale of products, often in large quantities, to retailers or other merchants.
2. the selling of goods to merchants; usually in large quantities for resale to consumers
*v.* 1. **批發, 經營批發業** — sell in large quantities
*adj.* 1. ignoring distinctions
*“sweeping generalizations”*
*“wholesale destruction”*
*adv.* 1. at a wholesale price
*“I can sell it to you wholesale”*
2. on a large scale without careful discrimination
*“I buy food wholesale”*

<a id="entry-w-001338"></a>
## wholesale house

*n.* 1. **廉價零售商** — a sales outlet offering goods at a discounted price

<a id="entry-w-001339"></a>
## wholesaler

*n.* 1. **批發商 /批發商** — A person or company that buys merchandise from manufacturers, importers, or distributors and resells the merchandise to retail businesses and to business and institutional end users.
2. someone who buys large quantities of goods and resells to merchants rather than to the ultimate customers

<a id="entry-w-001340"></a>

**發音：** /ˈhoʊl.səm/

*“wholesome attitude”*
*“wholesome appearance”*
2. **治癒的，治癒系** — Promoting good physical health and well-being.
3. sound or exhibiting soundness in body or mind
*“exercise develops wholesome appetites”*
*“a grin on his ugly wholesome face”*

*“he was wholly convinced”*

*“The children whooped when they were led to the picnic table”*
*n.* 1. a loud hooting cry of exultation or excitement

*“The members of the wedding party made merry all night”*
*“Let's whoop it up--the boss is gone!”*

2. **百日咳** — a disease of the respiratory mucous membrane

## wick

**發音：** /ˈwɪk/
*n.* 1. *(broadly, countable)* **燈芯 /燈芯, 燈心 /燈心, 炷, 燭心 /燭心** — Any piece of porous material that conveys liquid by capillary action; specifically (medicine), a strip of gauze placed in a wound, etc., to absorb fluids.
2. any piece of cord that conveys liquid by capillary action
*“the physician put a wick in the wound to drain it”*
3. a loosely woven cord (in a candle or oil lamp) that draws fuel by capillary action up into the flame

<a id="entry-w-001364"></a>

3. **邪惡 /邪惡, 調皮 /調皮** — Evil or mischievous by nature; morally reprehensible.

5. **惡作劇+的, 頑皮+的, 淘氣+的** — naughtily or annoyingly playful
*“teasing and worrying with impish laughter”*

*“severe pain”*

<a id="entry-w-001369"></a>
## wide

**發音：** /waɪd/
*adj.* 1. **寬+的, 寬廣, 廣+的, 寬闊+的** — having great (or a certain) extent from one side to the other
*“wide roads”*
*“a wide necktie”*
2. **包括一切+的, 廣大+的, 廣闊+的, 廣泛+的, 寬闊+的** — broad in scope or content
*“across-the-board pay increases”*
*“an all-embracing definition”*
3. **寬 /寬, 寬廣的 /寬廣的, 寬闊的 /寬闊的** — Having a large physical extent from side to side.
4. **大+的** — great in degree
*“won by a wide margin”*
5. (used of eyes) fully open or extended
*“stared with wide eyes”*
6. very large in expanse or scope
*“a broad lawn”*
*“the wide plains”*
*adv.* 1. with or by a broad space
*“stand with legs wide apart”*
*“ran wide around left end”*
2. to the fullest extent possible
*“open your eyes wide”*
*“with the throttle wide open”*
3. far from the intended target
*“the arrow went wide of the mark”*
*“a bullet went astray and killed a bystander”*
4. to or over a great extent or range; far
*“wandered wide through many lands”*
*“he traveled widely”*

**發音：** /ˈwaɪdn̩/

*“The law was extended to all citizens”*

*“the West's far-flung mountain ranges”*

<a id="entry-w-001413"></a>
## wild

**發音：** /ˈwaɪ̯ld/
*adj.* 1. **失控+的, 無節制+的, 無法無天+的** — marked by extreme lack of restraint or control
*“wild talk”*
*“wild parties”*
2. **未經馴服+的, 未馴服+的, 生長或生活在自然狀態下+的, 野+的, 野生+的** — in a natural state; not tamed or domesticated or cultivated
*“wild geese”*
*“edible wild plants”*
3. **狂野** — Raucous, unruly, or licentious.
4. **無根據+的, 無稽+的** — without a basis in reason or fact
*“baseless gossip”*
*“the allegations proved groundless”*
5. **狂亂+的, 語無倫次+的** — talking or behaving irrationally
*“a raving lunatic”*
6. **冒危險+的, 冒險+的, 擔風險+的, 危險+的, 有危險+的** — involving risk or danger
*“skydiving is a hazardous sport”*
*“extremely risky going out in the tide and fog”*
*adv.* 1. **無節制+地, 猖獗+地, 猛烈+地, 瘋狂+地, 狂暴+地** — in an uncontrolled and rampant manner
*“weeds grew rampantly around here”*
2. in a wild or undomesticated manner
*“growing wild”*
*“roaming wild”*
*n.* 1. **原野, 天然** — a wild primitive state untouched by civilization
*“he lived in the wild”*
*“they collected mushrooms in the wild”*
2. **荒原, 荒地, 荒野** — a wild and uninhabited area left in its natural condition
*“it was a wilderness preserved for the hawks and mountaineers”*

## wildcat

**發音：** /ˈwaɪldˌkæt/
*n.* 1. **野貓** — any small or medium-sized cat resembling the domestic cat and living in the wild
2. *(UK)* **斑貓 /斑貓, 野貓 /野貓, 山貓 /山貓** — Felis silvestris, a common small Old World wild cat somewhat larger than a house cat.
3. an exploratory oil well drilled in land not known to be an oil field
4. a cruelly rapacious person
*adj.* 1. **有風險+的** — outside the bounds of legitimate or ethical business practices
*“wildcat currency issued by irresponsible banks”*
*“wildcat stock speculation”*
2. without official authorization
*“an unauthorized strike”*
*“wildcat work stoppage”*
3. (of a mine or oil well) drilled speculatively in an area not known to be productive
*“drilling there would be strictly a wildcat operation”*
*“a wildcat mine”*

2. **荒原, 荒地, 荒野** — a wild and uninhabited area left in its natural condition
*“it was a wilderness preserved for the hawks and mountaineers”*

## will

**發音：** /wɪl/
*n.* 1. **決心, 決斷力, 意志, 意志力** — the capability of conscious choice and decision and intention
*“the exercise of their volition we construe as revolt"- George Meredith”*

*“where there's a will there's a way”*
*v.* 1. *(auxiliary, no-past-participle)* **將 /將, 要, 將要 /將要, 會 /會** — Used to express the future tense, sometimes with an implication of volition or determination when used in the first person. Compare shall.

*“My aunt bequeathed me all her jewelry”*
*“My grandfather left me his entire estate”*

4. determine by choice
*“This action was willed and intended”*

## wind

*n.* 1. **氣流, 風** — air moving (sometimes with considerable force) from an area of high pressure to an area of low pressure
*“trees bent under the fierce winds”*
*“when there is no wind, row”*
2. **預兆** — an indication of potential opportunity
*“he got a tip on the stock market”*
*“a good lead for a job”*
3. **卷繞, 扭, 擰, 纏繞, 卷** — the act of winding or twisting
*“he put the key in the old clock and gave it a good wind”*
4. a tendency or force that influences events
*“the winds of change”*
5. breath
*“the collision knocked the wind out of him”*
6. empty rhetoric or insincere or exaggerated talk
*“that's a lot of wind”*
*“don't give me any of that jazz”*
*v.* 1. **卷繞, 盤繞, 彎曲** — extend in curves and turns
*“The road winds around the lake”*
*“the path twisted through the forest”*
2. **嗅, 嗅到, 聞, 聞到, 嗅出** — catch the scent of; get wind of
*“The dog nosed out the drugs”*
3. **上發條** — coil the spring of (some mechanical device) by turning a stem
*“wind your watch”*
4. to move or cause to move in a sinuous, spiral, or circular course
*“the river winds through the hills”*
*“the path meanders through the vineyards”*
5. arrange or or coil around
*“roll your hair around your finger”*
*“Twine the thread around the spool”*
6. form into a wreath

<a id="entry-w-001557"></a>
## wind up

*v.* 1. **最終成為, 結果成為** — finally be or do something
*“He ended up marrying his high school sweetheart”*
*“he wound up being unemployed and living at home again”*
2. **上發條** — coil the spring of (some mechanical device) by turning a stem
*“wind your watch”*
3. give a preliminary swing to the arm pitching
4. stimulate sexually
*“This movie usually arouses the male audience”*

2. a kind of heavy jacket (`windcheater' is a British term)

<a id="entry-w-001560"></a>
## windfall

**發音：** /ˈwɪnd.fɔːl/
*n.* 1. *(figuratively)* **意外之財 /意外之財, 暴利** — A sudden large benefit; especially, a sudden or unexpected large amount of money, as from lottery or sweepstakes winnings or an unexpected inheritance or gift.
2. fruit that has fallen from the tree
3. a sudden happening that brings good fortune (as a sudden opportunity to make money)
*“the demand for testing has created a boom for those unregulated laboratories where boxes of specimen jars are processed like an assembly line”*

<a id="entry-w-001561"></a>

<a id="entry-w-001562"></a>

## window

*n.* 1. **最優時間, 最佳時機, 機會期** — the time period that is considered best for starting or finishing something
*“the expanded window will give us time to catch the thieves”*
*“they had a window of less than an hour when an attack would have succeeded”*
2. **窗口, 窗戶** — an opening in a wall or screen that admits light and air and through which customers can be served
*“he stuck his head in the window”*
3. a framework of wood or metal that contains a glass windowpane and is built into a wall or roof to admit light or air
4. a transparent opening in a vehicle that allow vision out of the sides or back; usually is capable of being opened
5. a transparent panel (as of an envelope) inserted in an otherwise opaque material
6. an opening that resembles a window in appearance or function
*“he could see them through a window in the trees”*

## wink

**發音：** /ˈwɪŋk/
*v.* 1. **使眼色, 眨, 眨眼睛, 眨眼** — signal by winking

2. **閃光, 閃爍, 閃耀** — gleam or glow intermittently
*“The lights were flashing”*

*“The TV announcer never seems to blink”*

5. **眨眼以除去** — force to go away by blinking
*“blink away tears”*
*n.* 1. **一眨眼的功夫, 一瞬間, 片刻, 眨眼間, 霎時** — a very short time (as the time it takes the eye to blink or the heart to beat)

2. closing one eye quickly as a signal
3. a reflex that closes and opens the eyes rapidly

## winnow

**發音：** /ˈwɪnoʊ/
*v.* 1. *(transitive)* **簸** — To subject (granular material, especially food grain) to a current of air separating heavier and lighter components, as grain from chaff.
2. separate the chaff from by using air currents
*“She stood there winnowing chaff all day in the field”*
3. blow on
*“The wind was winnowing her hair”*
*“the wind winnowed the grass”*
4. select desirable parts from a group or list
*“cull out the interesting letters from the poet's correspondence”*
*“winnow the finalists from the long list of applicants”*
5. blow away or off with a current of air
*“winnow chaff”*
*n.* 1. **箕** — That which winnows or which is used in winnowing; a contrivance for fanning or winnowing grain.
2. the act of separating grain from chaff
*“the winnowing was done by women”*

<a id="entry-w-001640"></a>

*“This possibility can be eliminated from our consideration”*

## wish

**發音：** /ˈwɪʃ/
*v.* 1. *(intransitive)* **願望 /願望, 希望, 但願 /但願** — To hope (for a particular outcome), even if that outcome is unlikely to occur or cannot occur.
2. *(ditransitive)* **祝** — To bestow (a thought or gesture) towards (someone or something).
3. hope for; have a wish
*“I wish I could go home now”*
4. prefer or wish to do something
*“Do you care to try this dish?”*
*“Would you like to come along to the movies?”*
5. make or express a wish
*“I wish that Christmas were over”*
6. feel or express a desire or hope concerning the future or fortune of
*n.* 1. **願望 /願望** — A desire, hope, or longing for something or for something to happen.
2. a specific feeling of desire
*“he got his wish”*
*“he was above all wishing and desire”*
3. an expression of some desire or inclination
*“I could tell that it was his wish that the guests leave”*
*“his crying was an indirect request for attention”*
4. (usually plural) a polite expression of desire for someone's welfare
*“give him my kind regards”*
*“my best wishes”*
5. the particular preference that you have
*“it was his last wish”*
*“they should respect the wishes of the people”*

**發音：** /ˈwɪtʃ ˌdɒktə/
*n.* 1. **巫醫 /巫醫** — A person (often male) who is believed to ward off witchcraft and heal through magical powers; a shaman.
2. someone who is believed to heal through magical powers

<a id="entry-w-001713"></a>
## witch-hunt

**發音：** /ˈwɪtʃhʌnt/
*n.* 1. *(historical)* **獵巫 /獵巫, 獵女巫 /獵女巫** — A search for people believed to be using sorcery or harmful magic, typically in order to persecute or punish them.
2. **對行巫者的搜捕, 獵女巫** — searching out and harassing dissenters
3. *(broadly, figuratively)* **政治迫害** — An attempt to find and punish or harass a group of people perceived as a threat, usually on ideological or political grounds.

<a id="entry-w-001714"></a>
## witchcraft

**發音：** /ˈwɪt͡ʃkɹæft/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **巫術 /巫術, 魔術 /魔術, 魔法** — The practice of witches; magic, sorcery, or the use of supernatural powers to influence or predict events.
2. the art of sorcery
