*“the last thing in swimwear”*
2. having the shrewd resourcefulness needed to survive in an urban environment

<a id="entry-w-001728"></a>
## withdraw

**發音：** /wɪðˈdɹɔː/
*v.* 1. **向後移, 退避, 撤退, 退卻, 離開** — pull back or move away or backward
*“The enemy withdrew”*
*“The limo pulled away from the curb”*
2. **撤退, 離開** — withdraw from active participation
*“He retired from chess”*
3. **召回, 撤回, 取回** — cause to be returned
*“recall the defective auto tires”*
*“The manufacturer tried to call back the spoilt yoghurt”*
4. **撤回, 收回** — take back what one has said
*“He swallowed his words”*
5. **提款, 取出, 拿出, 取回** — remove (a commodity) from (a supply source)
*“She drew $2,000 from the account”*
*“The doctors drew medical supplies from the hospital's emergency bank”*
6. *(transitive)* **提款** — To extract (money) from a bank account or other financial deposit.

<a id="entry-w-001729"></a>
## withdrawal

**發音：** /wɪðˈdɹɔː(ə)l/
*n.* 1. **撤銷先前的觀點, 收回** — a retraction of a previously held position
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **退出, 撤退** — Receiving from someone's care what one has earlier entrusted to them. Usually refers to money.
3. **退出, 脫離, 分離** — formal separation from an alliance or federation
4. *(countable, uncountable)* **戒斷症狀 /戒斷症狀** — A type of metabolic shock the body undergoes when a substance (such as a drug) on which a patient is dependent is withheld.
5. *(countable, uncountable)* **提款, 撤退** — An act of withdrawing or a state of being withdrawn.
6. **孤僻, 沉默寡言** — avoiding emotional involvement

<a id="entry-w-001730"></a>
## withdrawal symptom

*n.* 1. *(especially, in-plural)* **退癮症狀 /退癮症狀, 戒斷症狀 /戒斷症狀, 停藥症狀 /停藥症狀** — Any of a wide range of symptoms associated with the discontinuance of a drug by an addicted individual, or the medically controlled discontinuance by a patient of a prescribed drug.
2. **斷癮症狀, 禁戒症狀** — any physical or psychological disturbance (as sweating or depression) experienced by a drug addict when deprived of the drug

3. *(transitive)* **據為己有** — To keep (a physical object that one has obtained) to oneself rather than giving it back to its owner.
4. *(transitive)* **隱瞞** — To keep (information, assent, etc.) to oneself rather than revealing it.

## word

*n.* 1. **單詞, 字詞, 詞** — a unit of language that native speakers can identify
*“words are the blocks from which sentences are made”*
*“he hardly said ten words all morning”*
2. **情報, 報導, 新聞, 消息** — information about recent and important events
*“they awaited news of the outcome”*
3. **諾言** — a promise
*“he gave his word”*
4. **字** — a word is a string of bits stored in computer memory
*“large computers use words up to 64 bits long”*
5. **口令, 密碼, 暗號** — a secret word or phrase known only to a restricted group
*“he forgot the password”*
6. **聖經, 基督教神聖的著作** — the sacred writings of the Christian religions
*“he went to carry the Word to the heathen”*
*v.* 1. **表達** — put into words or an expression

<a id="entry-w-001949"></a>
## work

*v.* 1. **勞動** — exert oneself by doing mental or physical work for a purpose or out of necessity
*“I will work hard to improve my grades”*
*“she worked hard for better living conditions for the poor”*
2. **做工, 工作, 做** — be employed
*“Is your husband working again?”*
*“My wife never worked”*
3. **有效, 起作用** — have an effect or outcome; often the one desired or expected
*“The voting process doesn't work as well as people thought”*
*“How does your idea work in practice?”*
4. **使工作, 使運動** — give a workout to
*“Some parents exercise their infants”*
*“My personal trainer works me hard”*
5. **朝某方向走去** — proceed along a path
*“work one's way through the crowd”*
*“make one's way into the forest”*
6. **工作** — operate in a certain place, area, or specialty
*“She works the night clubs”*
*“The salesman works the Midwest”*
*n.* 1. **勞動, 職業, 工作** — the occupation for which you are paid
*“he is looking for employment”*
*“a lot of people are out of work”*
2. **工作場所, 職場** — a place where work is done
*“he arrived at work early today”*
3. **全部作品, 全集** — the total output of a writer or artist (or a substantial part of it)
*“he studied the entire Wagnerian oeuvre”*
*“Picasso's work can be divided into periods”*
4. activity directed toward making or doing something
*“she checked several points needing further work”*
5. a product produced or accomplished through the effort or activity or agency of a person or thing
*“it is not regarded as one of his more memorable works”*
*“the symphony was hailed as an ingenious work”*
6. applying the mind to learning and understanding a subject (especially by reading)
*“mastering a second language requires a lot of work”*
*“no schools offer graduate study in interior design”*

## work out

**發音：** /ˌwɜːk ˈaʊt/
*v.* 1. **起作用, 解決** — happen in a certain way, leading to, producing, or resulting in a certain outcome, often well
*“Things worked out in an interesting way”*
*“Not everything worked out in the end and we were disappointed”*
2. *(transitive)* **計算 /計算, 算出** — To calculate.
3. *(transitive)* **解釋 /解釋, 理解** — To make sense of.
4. **求解, 解決** — find the solution to (a problem or question) or understand the meaning of
*“did you solve the problem?”*
*“Work out your problems with the boss”*
5. **使工作, 使運動** — give a workout to
*“Some parents exercise their infants”*
*“My personal trainer works me hard”*
6. *(intransitive)* **鍛鍊** — To exercise, especially by lifting weights.

<a id="entry-w-001958"></a>
## work permit

*n.* 1. **工作許可 /工作許可** — A legal authorization which allows a person to take employment in a country other than the one in which they hold citizenship.
2. a legal document giving information required for employment of certain people in certain countries

<a id="entry-w-001959"></a>
## work product

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **工作成果, 工作產品 /工作產品** — Things and ideas created by an employee that are considered the legal property of the employer owing to the terms of employment

<a id="entry-w-001960"></a>
## work shift

*n.* 1. **工作班次, 班次工作時間** — the time period during which you are at work

<a id="entry-w-001961"></a>
## work shoe

*n.* 1. **笨重的鞋子** — a thick and heavy shoe

<a id="entry-w-001962"></a>
## work someone's ass off

*v.* 1. *(US)* **拼命工作, 做牛做馬 /做牛做馬** — (idiomatic, vulgar, slang) To work, or cause to work, excessively or to the point of exhaustion; see work + someone's ass off.

<a id="entry-w-001963"></a>
## work stoppage

*n.* 1. **停工, 罷工** — a group's refusal to work in protest against low pay or bad work conditions
*“the strike lasted more than a month before it was settled”*

<a id="entry-w-001964"></a>
## work unit

*n.* 1. **能量單位** — a unit of measurement for work

<a id="entry-w-001965"></a>
## work-life balance

**發音：** /ˈwɜː(ɹ)k ˈlaɪf ˈbæləns/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **工作與生活平衡 /工作與生活平衡, 勞逸平衡 /勞逸平衡** — The effective management and equilibrium between the demands of one's work or professional responsibilities and one's personal life.

<a id="entry-w-001966"></a>
## work-shy

*adj.* 1. **怕工作+的, 怠惰+的, 懶, 懶惰+的, 無所事事+的** — disinclined to work or exertion
*“faineant kings under whose rule the country languished”*
*“an indolent hanger-on”*

<a id="entry-w-001967"></a>
## work-study

*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **勤工儉學 /勤工儉學, 勤工助學 /勤工助學** — Of or relating to a program that allows university students to work part-time while attending school.

<a id="entry-w-001968"></a>
## workable

*adj.* 1. **可使用+的, 可做, 可實行+的, 可執行+的, 可用+的** — capable of being done with means at hand and circumstances as they are

<a id="entry-w-001969"></a>
## workaday

**發音：** /ˈwɜːkədeɪ/
*adj.* 1. **日常, 日用** — Suitable for everyday use.
2. **平凡, 普通** — Mundane or commonplace.
3. found in the ordinary course of events
*“a placid everyday scene”*
*“it was a routine day”*

<a id="entry-w-001970"></a>
## workaholic

*n.* 1. **工作狂, 拼命三郎** — A person who feels compelled to work excessively, including while at home.
2. person with a compulsive need to work
*adj.* 1. **工作狂的** — In the nature or manner of a workaholic.

<a id="entry-w-001971"></a>
## workbench

**發音：** /ˈwɝk.bɛnt͡ʃ/
*n.* 1. **工作臺** — a strong worktable for a carpenter or mechanic
2. **工作臺 /工作臺** — A sturdy bench or table at which manual work is done by a carpenter, machinist, etc.

<a id="entry-w-001972"></a>
## workbook

**發音：** /ˈwɝk.bʊk/
*n.* 1. **練習冊 /練習冊** — A book, used by a student, in which answers and workings may be entered besides questions and exercises.
2. a student's book or booklet containing problems with spaces for solving them

<a id="entry-w-001973"></a>
## workday

*n.* 1. *(US)* **工作日** — Any of the days of a week on which work is done; any day in a workweek.
2. a day on which work is done
3. the amount of time that a worker must work for an agreed daily wage
*“they work an 8-hour day”*

<a id="entry-w-001974"></a>
## worked up

*adj.* 1. *(idiomatic, informal)* **情緒化 /情緒化** — Excessively emotional, excited, aroused or angry.
2. (of persons) excessively affected by emotion
*“he would become emotional over nothing at all”*
*“she was worked up about all the noise”*

<a id="entry-w-001975"></a>
## worker

**發音：** /ˈwɜː.kə/
*n.* 1. **勞動者, 工作者, 勞工, 工人** — a person who works at a specific occupation
*“he is a good worker”*
2. **工蟻, 工蜂** — sterile member of a colony of social insects that forages for food and cares for the larvae
3. **工人, 工作者, 勞動者 /勞動者, 勞工** — One who works: a person who performs labor for a living; traditionally, especially, manual labor.
4. **實幹家, 行為者** — a person who acts and gets things done
*“he's a principal actor in this affair”*
*“when you want something done get a doer”*
5. a member of the working class (not necessarily employed)
*“workers of the world--unite!”*

<a id="entry-w-001976"></a>
## worker bee

*n.* 1. **工蜂** — sterile bee specialized to collect food and maintain the hive
2. **工蜂** — A sterile bee whose specialty is to support the hive, especially by collecting pollen.

<a id="entry-w-001977"></a>
## workers of the world, unite

*phr.* 1. **全世界無產者，聯合起來 /全世界無產者, 聯合起來, 全方貧工之聯合 /全方貧工之聯合, 萬國勞動者團結起來呵 /萬國勞動者團結起來呵, 全世界無產階級和被壓迫的民族聯合起來 /全世界無產階級和被壓迫的民族聯合起來** — Laborers are encouraged to unionize or otherwise engage in collective action on a global scale in order to use the strength of their numbers to obtain better working conditions.

<a id="entry-w-001978"></a>
## workers' compensation

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **工傷賠償 /工傷賠償** — Insurance that pays for medical costs resulting from injuries sustained by employees in the workplace.

<a id="entry-w-001979"></a>
## workers' party

*n.* 1. **工人黨 /工人黨** — A political party generally claiming to represent and/or fight for the working class.

<a id="entry-w-001980"></a>
## workfellow

*n.* 1. **一起工作的人, 同事, 同僚** — an associate that one works with

<a id="entry-w-001981"></a>
## workflow

**發音：** /ˈwɜːkfləʊ/
*n.* 1. **工作流** — The flowrate at which a flow of work takes place.
2. *(especially)* **工作流程** — A process or procedure by which tasks are completed. It may be defined by a flowchart with actors, actions, results, decisions, and action paths.
3. progress (or rate of progress) in work being done

<a id="entry-w-001982"></a>
## workforce

**發音：** /ˈwɝk.foɹs/
*n.* 1. **勞動力 /勞動力, 員工 /員工** — All the workers employed by a specific organization or state, or on a specific project.
2. the force of workers available

<a id="entry-w-001983"></a>
## workhorse

*n.* 1. **役馬** — a horse used for plowing and hauling and other heavy labor
2. machine that performs dependably under heavy use
*“the IBM main frame computers have been the workhorse of the business world”*

<a id="entry-w-001984"></a>
## working capital

*n.* 1. **週轉資產, 流動資本, 營運資本, 營運資金** — assets available for use in the production of further assets
2. *(uncountable)* **營運資金 /營運資金** — The amount of capital a business or other entity has available for running its daily operations, calculated as current assets minus current liabilities.

<a id="entry-w-001985"></a>
## working capital requirement

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **營運資金需求 /營運資金需求** — The minimum amount of working capital that a business needs to run its operations smoothly.

<a id="entry-w-001986"></a>
## working class

*n.* 1. **工人階級 /工人階級, 勞動階級 /勞動階級** — The social class of those who perform physical work for a living, as opposed to the professional or middle class, the upper class, or others.
2. **一夥, 一堆, 勞動階級, 勞工, 工人階級** — a social class comprising those who do manual labor or work for wages
*“there is a shortage of skilled labor in this field”*

<a id="entry-w-001987"></a>
## working conditions

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **工作條件 /工作條件, 工作環境** — The environment in which one works, as influenced by factors such as cleanliness, lighting, equipment, paid overtime, uniforms, access to amenities, etc.

<a id="entry-w-001988"></a>
## working day

*n.* 1. *(UK, mainly)* **工作時間 /工作時間** — The part of a day in which work is done; the number of hours one must work per day for a specified wage.
2. a day on which work is done
3. the amount of time that a worker must work for an agreed daily wage
*“they work an 8-hour day”*

<a id="entry-w-001989"></a>
## working dog

*n.* 1. **工作犬, 役用犬** — Any breed of dog developed primarily to assist humans in performing tasks, such as farming, controlling livestock, law enforcement, or assisting the blind.
2. **役用犬** — any of several breeds of usually large powerful dogs bred to work as draft animals and guard and guide dogs

<a id="entry-w-001990"></a>
## working family

*n.* 1. **工人階級家庭 /工人階級家庭** — A family in the working class.

<a id="entry-w-001991"></a>
## working order

*n.* 1. *(especially, uncountable)* **正常運轉狀態 /正常運轉狀態, 正常工作** — The state or condition of being operational or of functioning acceptably.

<a id="entry-w-001992"></a>
## working poor

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **在職貧窮 /在職貧窮, 窮忙族 /窮忙族** — Working people whose incomes fall below a given poverty line.

<a id="entry-w-001993"></a>
## working-class

*adj.* 1. **工人階級+的, 工薪階層** — working for hourly wages rather than fixed (e.g. annual) salaries
*“working-class occupations include manual as well as industrial labor”*
2. of those who work for wages especially manual or industrial laborers
*“party of the propertyless proletariat"- G.B.Shaw”*

<a id="entry-w-001994"></a>
## workman

**發音：** /ˈwɝkmən/
*n.* 1. **工人** — A man who labours for wages.
2. an employee who performs manual or industrial labor

<a id="entry-w-001995"></a>
## workmanship

*n.* 1. **工藝, 手藝, 技藝, 技巧, 手段** — skill in an occupation or trade

<a id="entry-w-001996"></a>
## workout

**發音：** /ˈwɜːk.aʊ̯t/
*n.* 1. **體育鍛煉, 練習, 訓練, 鍛鍊, 運動** — the activity of exerting your muscles in various ways to keep fit
*“the doctor recommended regular exercise”*
*“he did some exercising”*
2. **鍛煉 /鍛鍊 or 鍛鍊 /鍛鍊** — An exercise session; a period of physical exercise.

<a id="entry-w-001997"></a>
## workpiece

**發音：** /ˈwɜːkpiːs/
*n.* 1. **工件** — A partially finished object (often composed of a raw material) which is subjected to various operations, worked on with tools, etc.
2. work consisting of a piece of metal being machined

<a id="entry-w-001998"></a>
## workplace

**發音：** /ˈwɝkˌpleɪs/
*n.* 1. **工作場所 /工作場所, 職場 /職場** — The place where someone works.
2. **工作場所, 職場** — a place where work is done
*“he arrived at work early today”*

<a id="entry-w-001999"></a>
## works

*n.* 1. **工廠** — buildings for carrying on industrial labor
*“they built a large plant to manufacture automobiles”*
2. **所有東西** — everything available; usually preceded by `the'
*“we saw the whole shebang”*
*“a hotdog with the works”*
3. performance of moral or religious acts
*“salvation by deeds”*
*“the reward for good works”*
4. the internal mechanism of a device

<a id="entry-w-002000"></a>
## worksheet

*n.* 1. **工作表** — A sheet of paper or computerized document on which problems are worked out or solved and the answers are recorded.
2. a sheet of paper with multiple columns; used by an accountant to assemble figures for financial statements
3. a piece of paper recording work planned or done on a project

<a id="entry-w-002001"></a>
## workshop

**發音：** /ˈwɜːk.ʃɒp/
*n.* 1. **工場, 車間** — small workplace where handcrafts or manufacturing are done
2. **專題討論會, 研討會, 講習會, 講習班** — a brief intensive course for a small group; emphasizes problem solving
3. **車間 /車間, 作坊, 工房, 工作坊** — A room, especially one which is not particularly large, used for manufacturing or other light industrial work.
4. **研討會 /研討會, 講習會 /講習會, 講習班 /講習班** — A brief, intensive course of education for a small group, emphasizing interaction and practical problem solving.
5. **研討會 /研討會** — An academic conference.

<a id="entry-w-002002"></a>
## workstation

**發音：** /ˈwɝkˌsteɪʃən/
*n.* 1. **工作站** — A desktop computer, normally more powerful than a normal PC and often dedicated to a specific task, such as graphics.
2. a desktop digital computer that is conventionally considered to be more powerful than a microcomputer

<a id="entry-w-002003"></a>
## workweek

*n.* 1. *(US)* **工作週 /工作周** — The range of days of the week that are normally worked.
2. hours or days of work in a calendar week
*“they worked a 40-hour week”*

<a id="entry-w-002004"></a>
## world

*n.* 1. **萬物, 太空, 宇宙, 世界** — everything that exists anywhere
*“they study the evolution of the universe”*
*“the biggest tree in existence”*
2. **個人生活的全部, 世界, 現實** — all of your experiences that determine how things appear to you
*“his world was shattered”*
*“we live in different worlds”*
3. **世界, 地球** — the 3rd planet from the sun; the planet we live on
*“the Earth moves around the sun”*
*“he sailed around the world”*
4. **世人, 平民** — people in general considered as a whole
*“he is a hero in the eyes of the public”*
5. **人類** — all of the living human inhabitants of the earth
*“all the world loves a lover”*
*“she always used `humankind' because `mankind' seemed to slight the women”*
6. people in general; especially a distinctive group of people with some shared interest
*“the Western world”*
*adj.* 1. involving the entire earth; not limited or provincial in scope
*“global war”*
*“global monetary policy”*

<a id="entry-w-002005"></a>
## World Bank

*prop. n.* 1. **世界銀行 /世界銀行** — A group of five financial organizations whose purpose is economic development and the elimination of poverty.

<a id="entry-w-002006"></a>
## World Cup

*n.* 1. **世界盃 /世界盃** — The FIFA World Cup, a quadrennial international competition of national teams in men's association football and the trophy awarded to the winning team.
2. **世界盃 /世界盃** — The trophy awarded to the winner of such an event.

<a id="entry-w-002007"></a>
## World Exposition

*n.* 1. **世博會 /世博會, 世界博覽會 /世界博覽會** — Any of various large international expositions held since the mid 19th century, often called "Expo".

<a id="entry-w-002008"></a>
## World Freedom Day

*prop. n.* 1. **一二三自由日** — an anticommunist memorial day celebrated on January 23 in Taiwan and South Korea

<a id="entry-w-002009"></a>
## World Health Assembly

*prop. n.* 1. **世界衛生大會 /世界衛生大會** — the forum through which the World Health Organization (WHO) is governed by its member states

<a id="entry-w-002010"></a>
## World Health Organization

*prop. n.* 1. **世界衛生組織 /世界衛生組織** — An agency of the United Nations responsible for international public health

<a id="entry-w-002011"></a>
## World Heritage Site

*n.* 1. **世界遺產 /世界遺產** — A place listed by UNESCO as being of special cultural or physical significance.

<a id="entry-w-002012"></a>
## world language

*n.* 1. **世界語言 /世界語言** — A language spoken internationally and which is learned by many people as a second language.

<a id="entry-w-002013"></a>
## world line

*n.* 1. **世界線 /世界線** — A path in spacetime, especially that traversed by an elementary particle from its creation to its destruction.

<a id="entry-w-002014"></a>
## world map

**發音：** /wɜːld ˈmæp/
*n.* 1. **世界地圖 /世界地圖** — A map of a world; a cartographic representation, a projection done to scale, representative of a planet's surface.

<a id="entry-w-002015"></a>
## world music

*n.* 1. *(countable, rare, uncountable)* **傳統音樂 /傳統音樂** — Traditional music, as opposed to popular music or classical music.
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **世界音樂 /世界音樂** — Music that combines elements from traditional styles from the non-Western world along with modern, popular elements, in order to create something marketed towards Western audiences.

<a id="entry-w-002016"></a>
## world order

*n.* 1. **世界秩序** — A particular structure that manages the world's stability, informed by a number of legal, political and socio-economic factors.

<a id="entry-w-002017"></a>
## world peace

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **世界和平** — The ideology of universal global understanding and non-violence.

<a id="entry-w-002018"></a>
## world power

*n.* 1. **世界強國 /世界強國, 大國 /大國** — A nation or state so powerful that it can influence world affairs.
2. **世界強國** — a state powerful enough to influence events throughout the world

<a id="entry-w-002019"></a>
## world record

*n.* 1. **世界紀錄 /世界紀錄** — The world's best score or time for a certain event.
2. the best record in the whole world

<a id="entry-w-002020"></a>
## World Trade Organization

*prop. n.* 1. **世界貿易組織 /世界貿易組織, WTO** — An international organization designed by its founders to supervise and liberalize international trade.

<a id="entry-w-002021"></a>
## World Turtle

*n.* 1. **鰲 /鰲** — The mythical giant sea turtle or tortoise that upholds the World in various cosmologies.

<a id="entry-w-002022"></a>
## world war

*n.* 1. **世界大戰 /世界大戰** — A war involving the major nations of the world.
2. a war in which the major nations of the world are involved

<a id="entry-w-002023"></a>
## world war 1

*n.* 1. **第一次世界大戰** — a war between the allies (Russia, France, British Empire, Italy, United States, Japan, Rumania, Serbia, Belgium, Greece, Portugal, Montenegro) and the Central Powers (Germany, Austria-Hungary, Turkey, Bulgaria) from 1914 to 1918

<a id="entry-w-002024"></a>
## world war 2

*n.* 1. **第二次世界大戰** — a war between the Allies (Australia, Belgium, Bolivia, Brazil, Canada, China, Colombia, Costa Rica, Cuba, Czechoslovakia, Dominican Republic, El Salvador, Ethiopia, France, Greece, Guatemala, Haiti, Honduras, India, Iran, Iraq, Luxembourg, Mexico, Netherlands, New Zealand, Nicaragua, Norway, Panama, Philippines, Poland, South Africa, United Kingdom, United States, USSR, Yugoslavia) and the Axis (Albania, Bulgaria, Finland, Germany, Hungary, Italy, Japan, Rumania, Slovakia, Thailand) from 1939 to 1945

<a id="entry-w-002025"></a>
## World War I

*prop. n.* 1. **第一次世界大戰 /第一次世界大戰, 一戰 /一戰** — The war from 1914 to 1918 between the Entente Powers of the British Empire, Russian Empire, France, Italy, the United States and other allied nations, against the Central Powers represented by the German Empire, Austria-Hungary, the Ottoman Empire, and Bulgaria.

<a id="entry-w-002026"></a>
## world war i

*n.* 1. **第一次世界大戰** — a war between the allies (Russia, France, British Empire, Italy, United States, Japan, Rumania, Serbia, Belgium, Greece, Portugal, Montenegro) and the Central Powers (Germany, Austria-Hungary, Turkey, Bulgaria) from 1914 to 1918

<a id="entry-w-002027"></a>
## World War II

**發音：** /ˌwɜː(ɹ)ld ˌwɔː(ɹ)ˈtuː/
*prop. n.* 1. *(historical)* **第二次世界大戰 /第二次世界大戰, 二戰 /二戰** — The war from 1939 to 1945 of the Allied forces, including the United Kingdom, the Soviet Union, the United States, France, and China, against the Axis Powers, including Germany, Italy, and Japan.

<a id="entry-w-002028"></a>
## world war ii

*n.* 1. **第二次世界大戰** — a war between the Allies (Australia, Belgium, Bolivia, Brazil, Canada, China, Colombia, Costa Rica, Cuba, Czechoslovakia, Dominican Republic, El Salvador, Ethiopia, France, Greece, Guatemala, Haiti, Honduras, India, Iran, Iraq, Luxembourg, Mexico, Netherlands, New Zealand, Nicaragua, Norway, Panama, Philippines, Poland, South Africa, United Kingdom, United States, USSR, Yugoslavia) and the Axis (Albania, Bulgaria, Finland, Germany, Hungary, Italy, Japan, Rumania, Slovakia, Thailand) from 1939 to 1945

<a id="entry-w-002029"></a>
## World War III

*prop. n.* 1. **第三次世界大戰 /第三次世界大戰, 三戰 /三戰** — A hypothetical world war following World War II.

<a id="entry-w-002030"></a>
## World Wide Web

**發音：** /ˈwɜːld ˌwaɪd ˈwɛb/
*prop. n.* 1. *(Internet)* **萬維網 /萬維網** — Usually preceded by the: all of the hypertext documents (web pages) on the Internet collectively, which are (1) stored in various inter-hyperlinked computers around the world, and (2) retrieved typically by means of the Hypertext Transfer Protocol (HTTP) or (currently more often) its secure version (HTTPS), among others.

<a id="entry-w-002031"></a>
## world-class

**發音：** /wɜː(ɹ)ldˈklæs/
*adj.* 1. **世界級 /世界級** — Of a standard that ranks among the best in the world.
2. ranking above all others
*“was first in her class”*
*“the foremost figure among marine artists”*

<a id="entry-w-002032"></a>
## world-famous

**發音：** /ˌwɜːldˈfeɪməs/
*adj.* 1. **舉世聞名 /舉世聞名** — Renowned universally; known worldwide.

<a id="entry-w-002033"></a>
## world-shaking

*adj.* 1. **驚天動地+的, 震撼世界+的** — sufficiently significant to affect the whole world
*“earthshaking proposals”*
*“the contest was no world-shaking affair”*

<a id="entry-w-002034"></a>
## world-shattering

*adj.* 1. **驚天動地+的, 震撼世界+的** — sufficiently significant to affect the whole world
*“earthshaking proposals”*
*“the contest was no world-shaking affair”*

<a id="entry-w-002035"></a>
## world-weariness

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **厭世 /厭世** — The state or characteristic of being world-weary; a feeling of fatigue or exhaustion with the state of the world or with one's life.
2. sadness on thinking about the evils of the world

<a id="entry-w-002036"></a>
## world-weary

*adj.* 1. **厭世+的** — tired of the world
*“bored with life”*
*“strolled through the museum with a bored air”*

<a id="entry-w-002037"></a>
## world-wide

*adj.* 1. **全世界+的, 全球+的, 國際+的, 普遍+的** — of worldwide scope or applicability
*“an issue of cosmopolitan import”*
*“the shrewdest political and ecumenical comment of our time"- Christopher Morley”*
2. involving the entire earth; not limited or provincial in scope
*“global war”*
*“global monetary policy”*
3. spanning or extending throughout the entire world
*“worldwide distribution”*
*“a worldwide epidemic”*

<a id="entry-w-002038"></a>
## worldly

*adj.* 1. **世間+的, 世俗+的** — characteristic of or devoted to the temporal world as opposed to the spiritual world
*“worldly goods and advancement”*
*“temporal possessions of the church”*
2. **厭煩於享樂+的** — very sophisticated especially because of surfeit; versed in the ways of the world
*“the blase traveler refers to the ocean he has crossed as `the pond'”*
*“the benefits of his worldly wisdom”*

<a id="entry-w-002039"></a>

*“I worry about the second Germanic consonant shift”*

**發音：** /ˈɹɒŋ/
*adj.* 1. **不正確+的, 不道德+的, 邪惡+的** — contrary to conscience or morality or law
*“it is wrong for the rich to take advantage of the poor”*
*“cheating is wrong”*
2. **出差錯+的, 有毛病+的** — not functioning properly
*“something is amiss”*
*“has gone completely haywire”*
3. **錯 /錯** — Incorrect or untrue.
4. **錯誤+的, 不正確+的** — based on or acting or judging in error
*“it is wrong to think that way”*
5. not correct; not in conformity with fact or truth
*“an incorrect calculation”*
*“the report in the paper is wrong”*
6. not appropriate for a purpose or occasion
*“said all the wrong things”*
*n.* 1. that which is contrary to the principles of justice or law
*“he feels that you are in the wrong”*
2. any harm or injury resulting from a violation of a legal right
*v.* 1. **不公正或不公道地對待, 冤枉, 委屈** — treat unjustly; do wrong to
2. **冤枉** — To slander; to impute evil to unjustly.
*adv.* 1. in an inaccurate manner
*“he decided to reveal the details only after other sources had reported them incorrectly”*
*“she guessed wrong”*

<a id="entry-w-002170"></a>
## wrong crowd

*n.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **壞人 /壞人, 壞孩子 /壞孩子** — A group of people deemed to have a negative influence on a person's behavior; a bad crowd.

<a id="entry-w-002171"></a>
## wrong-side-out

*adj.* 1. **裡面翻到外面+的** — with the inside surface on the outside

<a id="entry-w-002172"></a>
## wrongdoer

*n.* 1. **作惡者 /作惡者, 為非作歹者 /為非作歹者** — Someone who does wrong, whether morally, ethically, or in contravention of a law.
2. **做壞事的人, 罪犯** — a person who transgresses moral or civil law

<a id="entry-w-002173"></a>
## wrought

*adj.* 1. **製成一定形狀+的** — shaped to fit by or as if by altering the contours of a pliable mass (as by work or effort)
*“a shaped handgrip”*
*“the molded steel plates”*

<a id="entry-w-002174"></a>
## wrought iron

*n.* 1. **生鐵, 鑄鐵** — iron having a low carbon content that is tough and malleable and so can be forged and welded
2. **熟鐵 /熟鐵, 鍛鐵 /鍛鐵** — A tough, malleable, ductile form of iron that is forged rather than cast, suitable for welding.

*prep.* 1. *(abbreviation, alt-of, initialism)* **就……而言, 關於, 至於** — Initialism of with respect to, with regard to, or with reference to.

<a id="entry-w-002176"></a>
## wry

*adj.* 1. **扭歪+的, 歪斜+的** — bent to one side
*“a wry neck”*
2. humorously sarcastic or mocking
*“dry humor”*
*“an ironic remark often conveys an intended meaning obliquely”*
